<p> The Vancouver Sun - RSS Feedhttps : **27;38714;TOOLONG , 19 Mar 2020 19:41:06 +0000Lights out for local TV/film productions as COVID-19 forces shutdownhttp : **32;38743;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;38777;TOOLONG ... title= " On set of Van Helsing on Homer Street , between Dunsmuir and Georgia streets , in Vancouver . The hit Syfy Channel TV show is just one of the dozens of TV/film productions shut down in B.C. due to the COVID-19 virus . " alt= " On set of Van Helsing on Homer Street , between Dunsmuir and Georgia streets , in Vancouver . The hit Syfy Channel TV show is just one of the dozens of TV/film productions shut down in B.C. due to the COVID-19 virus . " data-portal-copyright= " Arlen Redekop " **28;38813;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1241168 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A week or so ago if you walked around a block in Downtown Vancouver there was a good chance you would see a member of a film crew pulling cable , setting lights and just generally getting on with the multibillion-dollar business of shows and films that are produced here in Hollywood North . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now , cut to this week , and the scene is very different as the &lt;a href= " https : **33;38843;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;COVID-19&lt;/a&gt; pandemic has pushed the industry to go dark . According to the &lt;a href= " http : //ubcp.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Union of B.C. Performers and ACTRA , &lt;/a&gt; all 42 of the productions they had listed are now shut down . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t know anybody who saw this coming , &amp;rdquo ; said Vancouver actor/producer/director &lt;a href= " https : **30;38878;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;John Cassini&lt;/a&gt; . &amp;ldquo ; I know when there is a writer&amp;rsquo ; s strike or something , you have a lot of lead up and people know , so , you tend to backload or front-load , whatever the term is . Everyone knows , &amp;lsquo ; **34;38910;TOOLONG ... before the strike hits . There&amp;rsquo ; s a little bit of planning . But , here in this case , people were just sent home from set or told it&amp;rsquo ; s not happening &amp;mdash ; all I think within 24-48 hours . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Things were just going , boom , boom , boom . That&amp;rsquo ; s it , full stop . That&amp;rsquo ; s it . There&amp;rsquo ; s no way any of us could have planned for this . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38946;TOOLONG ... title= " John Cassini said COVID-19 forced production shutdowns . Cassini lost a producing job and also has had to shutter his Railtown Actors Studio for the time being . " alt= " John Cassini said COVID-19 forced production shutdowns . Cassini lost a producing job and also has had to shutter his Railtown Actors Studio for the time being . " data-portal-copyright= " Gerry Kahrmann " **28;38982;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1242161 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cassini , who you can see now on Netflix in the classic Canadian and Vancouver-shot crime drama &lt;a href= " https : **31;39012;TOOLONG ? r ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **26;39045;TOOLONG , was set to start pre-production on a TV movie March 16 . That was tabled . The threat of spreading COVID-19 also led Cassini to temporarily close the doors on the non-profit Railtown Actors Studio that he co-founded eight years ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I thought this was going to be a bit of a sanctuary for everyone , &amp;rdquo ; said Cassini about the studio that has three instructors and teaches 70 students each term . &amp;ldquo ; I thought , &amp;lsquo ; OK , the business is going to shut down , maybe , and this is a place where we can come and really dig in to the work . And wait for the business to come back , &amp;rsquo ; but of course , thinking a little deeper it is now that the gatherings have to be smaller , and the gatherings are no more than 12 , 13 , 14 in a class sometimes . But still , I personally felt if I could do anything that&amp;rsquo ; s going to help this is it , besides staying home . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Van Helsing showrunner Jonathan Lloyd Walker of Vancouver returned from shooting the first three episodes of Season 5 of the hit Syfy channel show ready to begin filming the show&amp;rsquo ; s last 10 episodes in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It came on really quickly . Literally , this time last week we were coming back from Slovakia , &amp;rdquo ; said Walker . &amp;ldquo ; While we were shooting in Slovakia there was an awareness that it was happening in places in the world and it was happening on a fairly small scale , in terms of local impact . So , it didn&amp;rsquo ; t really affect us. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But all that changed in an instant when they returned to Vancouver on March 9. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; By the time we got to mid-week all of a sudden things were changing at such a pace I was like , &amp;lsquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t know what we are going to do ? &amp;rsquo ; It did come on very suddenly and took us all by surprise . Certainly we were monitoring the situation , then we reached the point when we just had to shut down . We made that decision , officially , over the weekend . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Walker&amp;rsquo ; s show , like many , will re-evaluate the situation in two-week&amp;rsquo ; s time . Others are waiting a month , and some have just ended their shoots early . While two weeks seems like a short period , it isn&amp;rsquo ; t in an industry that already comes packed with volatility and uncertainty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There are a lot people worried about their livelihood . For instance , a lot of actors and background performers I know that have said , &amp;lsquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve got five or six days of booked work and now I have nothing and I live paycheque-to-paycheque , what am I going to do ? &amp;rsquo ; Walker said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; From that standpoint , they are all nervous . They don&amp;rsquo ; t know how long the industry is going to be suspended . There is no work and there won&amp;rsquo ; t be for the foreseeable future . We&amp;rsquo ; re all going to have to calm some nerves and figure out a plan to take care of each other . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The plan now isn&amp;rsquo ; t clear . Industry unions have sent messages to the federal and provincial governments reminding them not to forget about the arts when it comes time to distribute emergency funding . Union websites have posts suggesting members seek out groups that help artists and apply for government benefits . A lot of actors , in particular , are self-employed so they don&amp;rsquo ; t qualify for unemployment benefits , however the Government of Canada just announced its COVID-19 Economic Response Plan . The plan will provide up to $27 billion in direct support to Canadian workers and businesses including those self-employed within the film industry . &amp;nbsp ; The unions have also told members that upcoming dues deadlines will be pushed back and reviewed over the course of this economic downturn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are dealing with the situation regarding COVID-19 as it unfolds in real time . With things changing so quickly we can&amp;rsquo ; t comment on any specifics . This is an unprecedented situation , not only locally but globally , and we continue to work in the best interest of the professional artists and technicians of the ( International Alliance of Theatrical Stage Employees ) Local 891 through this difficult time , &amp;rdquo ; said IATSE spokesman Phil Klapwyk in an email to Postmedia News . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The community , of course , reaches well beyond the faces you see on the screen . It literally takes a village to make a TV show or film . The tens-of-thousands of people the industry employs around B.C. include food-vendors , drivers , rental companies , special effects people , hair and makeup folks , and on and on . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In this industry , historically , people are always a bit jumpy about when the next job is coming because you don&amp;rsquo ; t always know . But , in the last few years , we have never had to really worry about that , &amp;rdquo ; said Vancouver TV/film hairstylist Jim Dreichel ( See and The Chilling Adventures of Sabrina ) , referring to the rebound in the industry after a slow period about seven years ago . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s been massive . Most of us turn down work all the time because we are already working . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Now there is nothing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Co-owner Glenn Ennis of Vancouver specialty camera team Peacemaker Filmworks ( Deadpool 1 &amp;amp ; 2 , The Predator , Arrow , Supergirl ) said the company wrapped a major car commercial March 15 , and now is looking at two months of nada. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39073;TOOLONG ... title= " Peacemaker Filmworks co-owner and stunt actor Glenn Ennis is one of the many , many people in the TV/film-production business in B.C. who have seen work disappear due to COVID-19. " alt= " Peacemaker Filmworks co-owner and stunt actor Glenn Ennis is one of the many , many people in the TV/film-production business in B.C. who have seen work disappear due to COVID-19. " data-portal-copyright= " NICK PROCAYLO " **28;39109;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1242225 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It has been resilient in that before you could just go somewhere else . Some other centre picks up the slack when somewhere else can&amp;rsquo ; t work for whatever reason . But this one is worldwide , and a complete unknown , &amp;rdquo ; said Ennis , who&amp;rsquo ; s also a stunt actor who has now lost a lot of work . &amp;ldquo ; A lot of people live paycheque-to-paycheque in the film world , depending on what part of it you are in and it can be pretty nasty . I mean , at the best of times you don&amp;rsquo ; t know when your next job is. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ennis and company are trying to figure out a way to keep some of their lights on and equipment running . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; At Peacemaker , we are trying to reach out and offer up our services to produce stuff in-house on our own . For instance , we can remotely do a car commercial for somebody , &amp;rdquo ; said Ennis , referring to a system that allows them to send footage over cellphone signals . This way a director can see footage , wherever they are , in real time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We could potentially go out and shoot a car commercial , which we have done a million times and we are good at it , and in real time people could be sitting in their agency office in New York and watching the filming and then by phone say , &amp;lsquo ; Yeah , that looks great but can you get it from another angle&amp;rsquo ; &amp;hellip ; We have done that before when a director was unavailable . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So yeah , we&amp;rsquo ; re trying to get people to look at other ways to do things in order to carry on , &amp;rdquo ; added Ennis. &amp;ldquo ; But , at this point , it is pretty early in the process to figure out what is going to evolve from this in the short-term . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Well-known Vancouver stage and screen actor Brian Markinson ( Mad Men , Girlfriends&amp;rsquo ; Guide to Divorce , the L Word , DaVinci&amp;rsquo ; s Inquest ) says he&amp;rsquo ; s lucky that his three-decade-long busy career has allowed him to save and buy a house , but he still feels the pinch when jobs are gone . He lost a gig thanks to this shutdown and remembers what it was like having to live paycheque-to-paycheque. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s tough , &amp;rdquo ; said Markinson , who has had to cancel teaching gigs , as well . &amp;ldquo ; But I know we will be there for each other . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39139;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver actor Brian Markinson hopes that out of the darkness of the COVID-19 crisis great art will be created . " alt= " Vancouver actor Brian Markinson hopes that out of the darkness of the COVID-19 crisis great art will be created . " data-portal-copyright= " Jeff Weddell Photography " **28;39175;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1241356 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over his 30-year career Markinson has read many , many scripts and doesn&amp;rsquo ; t hesitate when asked if this current crisis would be inspiration for a Hollywood movie . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think this is going to have to be a miniseries . I don&amp;rsquo ; t think a movie will hold it , &amp;rdquo ; said Markinson , adding that tough times have a history of inspiring artists to create . &amp;ldquo ; Out of these crises , that&amp;rsquo ; s when great art comes . Great scripts came out of the WPA ( U.S. New Deal Works Progress Administration ) and the Great Depression and the Vietnam War era . Shakespeare , it&amp;rsquo ; s said , wrote King Lear during the plague . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So hopefully , maybe great art will come out of this time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;39205;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;39232;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;39266;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeThu , 19 Mar 2020 19:41:06 **45;39297;TOOLONG ... **28;39344;TOOLONG : More Vancouver-based TV productions shut downhttp : **36;39374;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;39412;TOOLONG ... title= " A number of Vancouver TV productions are shutting down due to the COVID-19 outbreak . " alt= " A number of Vancouver TV productions are shutting down due to the COVID-19 outbreak . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;39448;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1155760 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;39478;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Vancouver film and television production industry is struggling as the number of productions shutting down due to the COVID-19 outbreak continues to grow . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Warner Bros . Television Group , which produces about a dozen TV series in Metro Vancouver , announced Friday that they were halting production on an unspecified number of their shows . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; With the rapidly changing events related to COVID-19 , and out of an abundance of caution , Warner Bros . Television Group is halting production on some of our 70+ series and pilots currently filming or about to begin . &amp;nbsp ; &amp;nbsp ; The health and safety of our employees , casts and crews remains our top priority . During this time , we will continue to follow the guidance of the Centers for Disease Control as well as local officials and public health professionals in each city where our productions are based , &amp;rdquo ; Warner Bros. said in a release . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The studio didn&amp;rsquo ; t say which series are being halted but &lt;a href= " https : **33;39514;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Hollywood Reporter&lt;/a&gt; says production has stopped on The CW shows The Flash , Supergirl and Batwoman , which all film in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The long-running CW show Supernatural , which is completing its 15th and final season , has also been shut down . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Warner Bros. had already shuttered production on the Metro Vancouver-shot Riverdale , The CW drama based on the Archie comics , earlier this week after a crew member came into contact with someone infected with COVID-19. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , Turner Network Television , which is also owned by Warner Media , confirmed Friday that production on the TNT series Snowpiercer has also been shut down . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;NETFLIX SHUTS DOWN PRODUCTION&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Netflix announced on Friday it was stopping all production of features and scripted series in the U.S .. and in Canada for at least two weeks . In Vancouver , that means the cameras stopped rolling on a yet-to-be-titled Sandra Bullock movie and the science fiction series Another Life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Production on the Mighty Ducks series for the Disney+ streaming service , which started filming in Vancouver last month , has been put on hiatus until April 5. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are currently more than 40 film and TV productions underway in B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver , the largest film and television production centre in Canada and the third in North America , had $3.6-billion worth of screen-based shows shot in the province during 2017-18. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;With files from Tiffany Crawford&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Scott BrownSat , 14 Mar 2020 04:41:48 **45;39549;TOOLONG ... **28;39596;TOOLONG Talk : Sweat Co. event helps folk here and in Sierra Leonehttp : **35;39626;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;39663;TOOLONG ... title= " Thirty-six years after founding her Sweat Co . Workout Studios in 1984 . Her Richards-off-Georgia facility is still there , and Wilson looked as she did on opening day while staging a recent event titled Miami Vice . That entailed double gender-bending in Shakespeare&amp;rsquo ; s day when men played female parts . Reversing that practice today , Kate Besworth will portray King Henry V in Bard in the Beach&amp;rsquo ; s June 10-Sept. 26 production . She and actor-husband Charlie Gallant attended a recent Bard benefit in Lorne and Melita Segal&amp;rsquo ; s Southlands home . Gallant will deliver Shakespeare&amp;rsquo ; s longest-ever speech , 77 lines , in the July 3-Sept. 26 staging of Love&amp;rsquo ; s Labour&amp;rsquo ; s Lost . No word on artistic director Christopher Gaze&amp;rsquo ; s mother Joy reprising her 1931 role in A Midsummer Night&amp;rsquo ; s Dream for Bard&amp;rsquo ; s June 10-Sept. 26 staging . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39795;TOOLONG ... title= " Afghanistan-born Mozhdah Jamalzadah stars in M ? t is director Marie Clements ' Red Snow that opened the Women in Film Vancouver Festival . Vancouver-born Galiano Island-resident Clements had the B.C. Interior stand in for Afghanistan in her film&amp;rsquo ; s depiction of a First Nations Canadian soldier captured by the Taliban . It premiered in cross-Canada theatres March 13 . During the festival , board president Shannon Kaplun said that Crown-corporation Telefilm was far from meeting the &amp;ldquo ; 50-50 by 2020&amp;rdquo ; campaign&amp;rsquo ; s target of equal funding for female-and male-directed feature films and TV series . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39861;TOOLONG ... title= " Susan Chow " alt= " Susan Chow " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;39897;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1236510 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39927;TOOLONG ... title= " Jane Young . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;40029;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1236513 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; FUTURE CARE : With the Chinese community&amp;rsquo ; s For Children We Care gala due on March 7 , organizers applied the brakes on March 5 . Mounting viral-epidemic concerns saw them postpone the 25th annual event at which co-chairs Susan Chow and Jane Young hoped to raise $5 million for the B.C. Children&amp;rsquo ; s Hospital Foundation . Icing such a close-to-fruition event necessarily incurred considerable cost , not least for the Fairmont Hotel Vancouver . However , the gala&amp;rsquo ; s characteristically beneficent participants , title sponsor Peterson Group and other supporters will doubtless rectify that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; ON HOLD : Viral concerns also rescheduled Jacqui Cohen&amp;rsquo ; s Face The World gala from May 30 to Sept. 12 when its 30-year benefits to **28;40059;TOOLONG ; s charities should top $20 million . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; LUCKY MAN : Nothing curtailed a March 5 event involving the Chinese Canadian community and others . That was a thanksgiving for Prospero International Realty founder and philanthropist Robert ( Bob ) Lee who died at age 86 on Feb. 19 . Attendees packed Christ Church Cathedral while others overflowed a Fairmont Hotel Vancouver reception room . The event entailed &amp;ldquo ; sadness , gratitude and joy , &amp;rdquo ; said Lee&amp;rsquo ; s brother Bill from CCC&amp;rsquo ; s pulpit . &amp;ldquo ; Bob in his character was not religious , &amp;rdquo ; Bill added . Then , acknowledging the locale : &amp;ldquo ; But he reflected the characteristics of disciples of Jesus . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40089;TOOLONG ... title= " Folk overflowed Christ Church Cathedral to join Lily Lee in a thanksgiving for **35;40125;TOOLONG Robert who died Feb. 19. " alt= " Folk overflowed Christ Church Cathedral to join Lily Lee in a thanksgiving for **35;40162;TOOLONG Robert who died Feb. 19. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;40199;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1236512 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Devout Christian Jim Pattison called Lee &amp;ldquo ; the most successful person I ever met &amp;hellip ; yet he still gave his time to serve his city and his country . &amp;rdquo ; Such service included sparkplugging the UBC Properties Trust that should raise $4 billion for his alma mater . Daughter Carol called Lee &amp;ldquo ; the poster boy for social entrepreneurship , &amp;rdquo ; and son Derek said : &amp;ldquo ; My Dad was lucky . &amp;rdquo ; Lee himself might have chuckled when the cathedral&amp;rsquo ; s PA system went silent during daughter Leslie&amp;rsquo ; s remarks . It likely recalled some of his cheery response to florid praise from former premier Gordon Campbell and others : &amp;ldquo ; Thank you for coming . I&amp;rsquo ; m going now . &amp;rdquo ; His casket left amid a peal of bells donated by the Jack and Darlene Foundation . Bob and wife Lily travelled often with the Pooles , Peter and Joanne Brown and Nelson and the late Eleni Skalbania . According to Brown , Lee remained &amp;ldquo ; humble , never beat his chest &amp;hellip ; and , in his final year , took all the family to the PNE to maintain a tradition . &amp;rdquo ; Son Derek had the right word for it : &amp;ldquo ; Lucky . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40229;TOOLONG ... title= " Dr. Daniel Kalla 's years heading St. Paul 's Hospital 's ER facility add texture to his 10th novel , The Last High , about the continuing opioid crisis . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;40265;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1236514 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; FIGHTING FENTANYL : This column recently expected COVID-19 to inspire another epidemic-oriented novel from city-based Daniel Kalla . Meanwhile , his 10th book , The Last High ( Simon &amp;amp ; Schuster Canada ) , addresses the fentanyl-driven opioid crisis that has killed close to 14,000 Canadians . Picturing the experiences of victims , dealers , police and especially hospital staff , Kalla provides absorbing procedural accuracy from his years heading emergency surgery at St. Paul&amp;rsquo ; s Hospital ( St. Michael&amp;rsquo ; s in the book ) . An amusing personal reference in the novel has toxicologist-ER doctor Julie Rees relate her fictional colleagues&amp;rsquo ; secondary interests : &amp;ldquo ; One guy even writes books &amp;mdash ; medical thrillers &amp;mdash ; they&amp;rsquo ; re not half bad . &amp;rdquo ; Not half bad , indeed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; DOWN PARRYSCOPE : I kiss the ground today , March 14 , the anniversary of my being welcomed to ever-peerless British Columbia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;40295;TOOLONG " **34;40324;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 604-929-8456 &lt;/p&gt; Malcolm ParryFri , 13 Mar 2020 14:06:10 **45;40360;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal BusinessLocal HealthMedicine MattersMoviesSmall **34;40407;TOOLONG music : Vancouver Symphony Orchestra pays tribute to Beethovenhttp : **31;40443;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;40476;TOOLONG ... title= " VSO composer-in residence Rodney Sharman 's Archaic Smile will feature in Beethoven The Modernist ( March 7 at the Orpheum ) " alt= " VSO composer-in residence Rodney Sharman 's Archaic Smile will feature in Beethoven The Modernist ( March 7 at the Orpheum ) " data-portal-copyright= " Andrew Shopland " **28;40512;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1222242 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;The Beethoven Experience&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; March 7&amp;ndash ; 21 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Various locations &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Info and tickets : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;40542;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **30;40571;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s Beethoven Year &amp;mdash ; the 250th anniversary of Ludwig van&amp;rsquo ; s birth . There are any number of special events designed to commemorate this , including the Vancouver Symphony&amp;rsquo ; s Beethoven Experience set of concerts and events March 7 through 21. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But , I hear you ask , isn&amp;rsquo ; t every classical music season a Beethoven year ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40603;TOOLONG ... title= " Ludwig van Beethoven . " alt= " Ludwig van Beethoven . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40639;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223982 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 1959 , the great Leonard Bernstein talked about our fixation with the composer in his book The Joy of Music . In one of his landmark essays he noted , with more than a hint of exasperation : &amp;ldquo ; Ever since I can recall , the first association that springs to anyone&amp;rsquo ; s mind when serious music is mentioned is &amp;lsquo ; Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; . When you walk into a concert hall bearing the names of the greats inscribed around it on a frieze , there he sits , front and centre , the first , the largest , the most immediately visible , and usually gold-plated. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yet Lenny had his answers about why Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s stature remains larger than life , and I have a few ideas too : he&amp;rsquo ; s that good , that radical , that human , that uplifting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Good thoughts to keep in mind during a year of multiple helpings of the great master&amp;rsquo ; s work , mostly good , sometimes bad , rarely indifferent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What has the VSO got in store for us , and how will it add to our understanding of LvB ? I think it&amp;rsquo ; s all about creating a nuanced understanding of context : what his music meant in his own time , and how his legacy evolved through the ages up to the present day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The centre of the Beethoven Experience is two concerts , &lt;strong&gt;A Most Remarkable Night , Parts 1 and 2&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( March 13 and 14 at the Orpheum ) &lt;/em&gt; . This is a two-evening duplication of a single early 19th century program the VSO describes as &amp;ldquo ; an astounding four-hour concert that saw the premi&amp;egrave ; res of both the 5th and 6th Symphonies , the Choral Fantasy , and his Piano Concerto No. 4 , along with an improvised piano fantasy &amp;mdash ; Beethoven conducting and playing all of it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This program is pragmatically stretched out over two evenings to suit 21st century audiences , for whom a four-hour-plus extravaganza would be too much of a good thing . Placing three masterworks in proximity enables a new way of hearing ultrafamiliar repertoire , just like conductor Otto Tausk&amp;rsquo ; s trilogy of late Mozart symphonies last season . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;In the Heart of the Symphony&lt;/strong&gt; ( &lt;em&gt;March 11 ) &lt;/em&gt; , takes the renowned Fifth Symphony to unfamiliar turf for the orchestra : the heritage Commodore Ballroom . The strategy is bring the audience up close and real personal with the orchestra , and I think it&amp;rsquo ; s a simple , great idea . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tracing how the Beethoven myth grew and mutated over time is another aspect of the Beethoven Experience . **27;40669;TOOLONG : Chamber Concert&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( March 9 , Christ Church Cathedral ) &lt;/em&gt; &amp;nbsp ; will include a performance of Brahms&amp;rsquo ; s landmark Piano Quintet . It demonstrates how Beethoven impressed the best and brightest of the mid-19th century composers , and also includes baritone Tyler Duncan singing Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s An die ferne Geliebte. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The decision to reprise former VSO composer-in residence Rodney Sharman&amp;rsquo ; s Archaic Smile in &lt;strong&gt;Beethoven The Modernist&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;em&gt; ( March 7 at the Orpheum ) &lt;/em&gt; , shows that contemporary figures still feel a connection to Beethoven as the grandfather ( I use the gender intentionally ! ) of the radical tradition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **25;40698;TOOLONG Eroica&lt;/strong&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;em&gt; ( March 20 and 21 at the Chan Centre ) , &lt;/em&gt; led by Australian-born composer , conductor and violist Brett Dean offers another object lesson in how today&amp;rsquo ; s composers still think of Beethoven as almost a contemporary . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dean&amp;rsquo ; s program includes the Eroica Symphony and his own Beethoven-themed Testament . Provocatively , and the concert starts with Die grosse Fuge &amp;mdash ; a contrapuntal behemoth originally designed as the ending of an already elephantine string quartet ; Beethoven&amp;rsquo ; s idiosyncratic homage to the fugue will played here by a full string orchestra . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As with the VSO New Music festivals , the hope is that audience members see themselves as participants rather than passive listeners . To that end , various discounted series pass options are on offer . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;40725;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;40767;TOOLONG ... about the VSO&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;40803;TOOLONG ... about classical **26;40839;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;40867;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Davis " **28;41489;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1223647 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Clements&amp;rsquo ; film will open the 15th annual Vancouver International Women in Film Festival , which takes place at the Vancity Theatre from March 3 to 8 . The film is one of nine feature films at the festival . Also on the program are seven documentaries and 26 short films . In the festival there are eight Canadian premieres . The Argentinian drama The Rabbit&amp;rsquo ; s House about a young girl living with activists in the mid seventies will have its world premiere at VIWFF. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s always an honour to be included in a festival that celebrates women filmmakers , &amp;rdquo ; said Clements , a Metis multiple disciplined artist who lives on Galiano Island . &amp;ldquo ; Being able to be in a community and to see other women&amp;rsquo ; s work and to see , you know , what they&amp;rsquo ; re making and see what stories they&amp;rsquo ; re really passionate about , I think it just feeds you . There&amp;rsquo ; s a feeling when you&amp;rsquo ; re at these festivals , yes you&amp;rsquo ; re there for your baby , but you really are being inspired and kind of motivated , propelled by what other artists are doing and how they got to do it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;41519;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;41555;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Put on by the Women in Film and Television Vancouver organization , the VIWFF not only celebrates great cinema made by women but it offers a full slate of supportive programming including panels , seminars , artist talks , pitch sessions , a screenplay competition , social events and an awards gala . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I really think it&amp;rsquo ; s just like an incubator of ideas and possibilities , &amp;rdquo ; added Clements . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The concept for Red Snow , which also gets it theatrical release in &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Ottawa , Vancouver and Toronto on March 13th , &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; goes back a decade to when Clements was looking at photos of Canadian soldiers in Afghanistan . In the pictures were often Pashtuns and it was the images of those ethnic Afghans that really struck Clements . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was looking at a photo essay of Canadian soldiers in Afghanistan . And I just started looking at the faces of the Indigenous people there and you know , in certain angles they looked like Indigenous people in Canada . This was really great . What would happen if these two ancient cultures that had survived so many wars were face to face ? And what does modern tribalism look like ? And how do we survive using our identity ? You know , as people ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film was shot in 20 days ( some of those days the temperature dropped to -51 degrees ) on location outside of Yellowknife and here in B.C. near Ashcroft , Kamloops and Cache Creek . Four languages are spoken in the film : Pashtun , English , Gwich'in and Inuvialuktun. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41591;TOOLONG ... title= " Galiano Island filmmaker Marie Clements ' latest film Red Snow was shot near Yellowknife ( seen here ) as well as in locations in the B.C. Her work in the theatre has earned her awards and nominations and her plays have been translated into different languages . Right now she is busy developing screenplays , making music videos , working on a four-part miniseries with the CBC and a documentary with the NFB. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everything&amp;rsquo ; s percolating . Everything&amp;rsquo ; s moving forward , &amp;rdquo ; said Clements . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Interactive digital media and community outreach components have been created as part of the Red Snow experience . Dirt Walkers and We Are Still the People will be performed live by a band of Afghan and Canadian Indigenous musicians including : &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Mozhah Jamalzadah , Wayne Lavallee , Marina Hasselberg , Edris Fayaz and Majid Qiyam&lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Telling Indigenous stories has always been the plan for Clements . I think we&amp;rsquo ; re just looking to consume what we don&amp;rsquo ; t know . The documentary is making its Canadian premiere here at VIWFF on March 7 . The film from local director Doreen Manuel looks at the B.C. First Nations leaders who joined together to reject Prime Minister Pierre Elliot Trudeau&amp;rsquo ; s proposed 1969 White Paper Policy . That policy was setting out to abolish all legal documents that had previously existed , including the Indian Act , and all treaties within the country . Before designing and advocating mass-wood highrises , Green assisted C&amp;eacute ; sar Pelli on Kuala Lumpur&amp;rsquo ; s reinforced-concrete Petronas Towers . At 452 metres , the 1996 structures were the world&amp;rsquo ; s tallest until 2004 . Green recalls clients nixing Pelli&amp;rsquo ; s original design because his tower cross-sections resembled the six-pointed Star of David . When redrawn with two more to suggest the Muslim Rub El Hizb symbol , and with further facets added , Pelli got the go-ahead . Green has given himself the same for a vegetarian-vegan book based on his lunchtime feeding of Michael Green Architecture&amp;rsquo ; s 65 staff . Newland does have a new movie , though . Real EstateLocal ArtsLocal BusinessLocal HealthMedicine MattersMoviesReal EstateWomenBlockbuster film Sonic the Hedgehog provided $37.5M boost to B.C.http : **36;42537;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42575;TOOLONG ... title= " Paramount Pictures ' Sonic the Hedgehog film injected $37.5 million into B.C. 's film economy during its 2018 production . " alt= " Paramount Pictures ' Sonic the Hedgehog film injected $37.5 million into B.C. 's film economy during its 2018 production . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42611;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1217255 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many say you&amp;rsquo ; ve got to spend money to make money , and blockbuster film Sonic the Hedgehog did just that here in B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Paramount Pictures film is racing its way to the top of the box office , but not before it spent $37.5 million in B.C. during its 2018 production . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; According to figures released Thursday by the Motion Picture Association of Canada , the production spent $22 million on local labour , creating 1,450 jobs for local cast and crew when it spent 53 days filming in B.C. two years ago ( figures in Canadian dollars unless otherwise noted ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The professional artists and technicians of IATSE Local 891 love a challenge and bringing this much loved video game character to the big screen fit the bill , &amp;rdquo ; said Phil Klapwyk with IATSE Local 891 , which represents film workers across B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Sonic the Hedgehog isn&amp;rsquo ; t just a creative and technical success ; it provided good jobs for many B.C. crew members . We&amp;rsquo ; re definitely hoping for a sequel ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; An additional $15.5 million was injected into the local economy for production-related goods and services , including $490,000 on catering , $1.6 million on hotels and accommodations , $350,000 on hardware and lumber , and $300,000 on wardrobe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Production for the film , which is based on the classic Sega video game franchise about a speedy blue hedgehog , took place across B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s South Coast , with the majority of its filming locations on Vancouver Island . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;42641;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;42677;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Campbell River&amp;rsquo ; s Shelter Point Distillery , Nanaimo&amp;rsquo ; s Diplomat Hotel , Belcarra&amp;rsquo ; s Camp Howdy and Squamish&amp;rsquo ; s Stawamus Chief Park all feature in the film . A key action sequence &amp;ndash ; which sees Jim Carrey&amp;rsquo ; s Dr. Ivo Robotnik chasing Sonic , voiced by Ben Schwartz , and his human companion played by James Marsden &amp;ndash ; was filmed in the area around Vancouver Island&amp;rsquo ; s Highway 19. &lt;a href= " https : **33;42713;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The span was closed for five days in August 2018&lt;/a&gt; to allow for production to stage a number of vehicle-related stunts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Filming in B.C. provided us with incredible local cast and crew , and beautiful locations for Sonic the Hedgehog , &amp;rdquo ; said Lee Rosenthal with Paramount Pictures . &amp;ldquo ; We found great partnership in the local business community , and they satisfied all of our production-related needs . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sonic the Hedgehog hit theatres on Feb. 14 and raked in US $70 million over that country&amp;rsquo ; s four-day Presidents Day long weekend , &lt;a href= " https : **33;42748;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;according to the Hollywood Reporter&lt;/a&gt; . The film also debuted to US $43 million from its first 40 international markets . The film cost US $87 million to make . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The movie had initially been forecast to bring in US $45 to $50 million domestically over the four-day period , but beat that projection with US $58 million over just three days . Analysts now say the film has a chance at grossing US $200 million domestically . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A post-credits sequence also hinted at a possible continuation of the Sonic film franchise , after introducing Miles &amp;ldquo ; Tails&amp;rdquo ; Prower , a sidekick to Sonic and featured character in the original video games . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " mailto:sip@postmedia.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;42783;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **25;42816;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **31;42843;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42876;TOOLONG ... title= " Actors James Marsden , Ben Schwartz , Jim Carrey and Tika Sumpter ( left to right ) at a Sonic The Hedgehog Family Day Event on Jan. 25 in Hollywood . " alt= " Actors James Marsden , Ben Schwartz , Jim Carrey and Tika Sumpter ( left to right ) at a Sonic The Hedgehog Family Day Event on Jan. 25 in Hollywood . ArtsLocal NewsMoviesFilm fest speaker to lift ' bad*** women climbers ' profile in the great outdoorshttp : **32;43230;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;43264;TOOLONG ... title= " Shelma Jun will kick off the Vancouver International Mountain Film Festival with an opening-night presentation at North Vancouver 's Centennial Theatre on Feb. 21. " alt= " Shelma Jun will kick off the Vancouver International Mountain Film Festival with an opening-night presentation at North Vancouver 's Centennial Theatre on Feb. 21. " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy , VIMFF " **28;43300;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1212802 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vancouver International Mountain Film Festival&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 21-March 1 . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Various locations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : //vimff.org/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;vimff.org&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rock climber and upcoming &lt;a href= " https : //vimff.org/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver International Mountain Film Festival ( VIMFF ) &lt;/a&gt; speaker &lt;a href= " https : **31;43330;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Shelma Jun&lt;/a&gt; sees the great outdoors as a great way to empower women . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Through her organization &lt;a href= " https : //flashfoxy.com/foxforce " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Flash Foxy&lt;/a&gt; and the &lt;a href= " https : //flashfoxy.com/wcf " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Women 's Climbing Festival ( WCF ) &lt;/a&gt; , the New Yorker is all about celebrating a growing community of &amp;ldquo ; badass women climbers . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A former urban planner and community organizer , Jun decided it would be great to get women together for an organized weekend of climbing , conversation and camaraderie . It turns out other women thought that was a great idea , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I thought there would be like 40 of us and it ended up being 200 , &amp;rdquo ; Jun said of the first event in Bishop , Calif. , in 2016. &amp;ldquo ; The Women&amp;rsquo ; s Climbing Festival has been amazing . It always sells out ( and ) there is several hundred women on the waiting list . The second year we had 800 on the wait list . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The WCF is held annually in two locations : Bishop and Chattanooga , Tenn. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since beginning Flash Foxy in 2014 , Jun has gone on to be an outspoken and successful advocate for women climbers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the ways she does that is by working with the American Mountain Guide Association to help create opportunities for women to become guides . That initiative is just part of Jun&amp;rsquo ; s goal to get more women outside and more women listened to once they do get outdoors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jun will be talking about the push for an equitable outdoors when she delivers her opening-night presentation at the VIMFF on Feb. 21 at 7:30 p.m. in North Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Centennial Theatre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I am really proud of creating a space that is challenging the status quo and creating opportunities for women , especially women from underrepresented groups , to have a voice in the outdoor space , &amp;rdquo ; Jun said recently from her home in Brooklyn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She also makes movies , and those gathered in North Vancouver for her festival appearance will get a look at her latest work , Duality . The short film follows Jun on a climbing trip to her native South Korea . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43363;TOOLONG ... title= " ' I am really proud of creating a space that is challenging the status quo and creating opportunities for women , especially women from underrepresented groups , to have a voice in the outdoor space , ' says Shelma Jun. " alt= " ' I am really proud of creating a space that is challenging the status quo and creating opportunities for women , especially women from underrepresented groups , to have a voice in the outdoor space , ' says Shelma Jun. " data-portal-copyright= " Sasha Turrentine " **28;43399;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213417 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The idea was , if I go Korea and climb with Korean climbers , what will I feel ? Will it feel different ? Will I feel more connected ? Or will it not ? &amp;rdquo ; said Jun , who moved from South Korea to California at age five . &amp;ldquo ; That was kind of really the premise of this trip . Obviously there were some unexpected discoveries and situations , so this presentation I am doing on opening night is really about my story of being Korean American , of being a climber going on this trip and kind of what I started to discover from that and what I learned from it and how I am feeling moving forward . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The short film follows Jun &amp;mdash ; who counts as one of her main sponsors North Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s high-performance outdoor equipment company Arc&amp;rsquo ; Teryx &amp;mdash ; all over South Korea , including a climb near the border with North Korea . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think it is a very relatable story of using the landscape to connect and just having multiple identities , &amp;rdquo ; said Jun. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;43429;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;43465;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Jun grew up in California , and after completing her masters degree in urban planning at UCLA moved to New York City about eight and half years ago . Once in NYC she decided to take up rock climbing . And yes , she sees the irony of leaving California only to fall in love with rock climbing while living in Brooklyn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She also discovered through climbing that she wanted and needed more meaningful relationships with women . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I found climbing here and an amazing group of women that I started climbing , learning and growing with , &amp;rdquo ; said Jun. &amp;ldquo ; I talked about it a little bit in other articles and things , but just kind of growing up a self-proclaimed tomboy I have always had these complicated relationships with women . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those relationships began to change with each rock faced scaled . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think it is the kind of partnerships that climbing allows . I think it is being in my 30s and I think it is having the women I have found here . I think having female mentors , I think it was a perfect storm of a lot of things in my life that really allowed me to appreciate this and value this group of women that I found through rock climbing and that&amp;rsquo ; s what kind of started Flash Foxy . It started out not meaning to be anything specific , just my girlfriends and I climbing and celebrating that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then this thing called Instagram really took off and suddenly Flash Foxy was 40,000 followers strong . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think a lot of people that thought they were the only ones discovered all of these people through Instagram , &amp;rdquo ; said Jun. &amp;ldquo ; They found people who were like them and enjoyed things they enjoyed . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43501;TOOLONG ... title= " Shelma Jun , shown climbing in South Korea last year . " alt= " Shelma Jun , shown climbing in South Korea last year . " data-portal-copyright= " Sasha Turrentine " **28;43537;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1213879 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jun feels climbing is the perfect activity to help encourage and empower women . Her festivals welcome women , ranging from those who don&amp;rsquo ; t know a carabiner from a nut to those who have a lifetime&amp;rsquo ; s worth of chalk under their fingernails . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s cool we can be all different levels and we can go out together and each of us can challenge ourselves and have a good time . That&amp;rsquo ; s one of the really special things about climbing , &amp;rdquo ; said Jun. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For her hard work , Jun in 2017 was named as one of Outside magazine&amp;rsquo ; s 40 women who have made the most impact on our world . In 2019 , Conde Nast Traveler put her on its 30 most powerful women in travel list . But those accolades , while nice , are not why she&amp;rsquo ; s in the game . She sees her life much like climbing , in that she makes one small move at a time , moves that will result in a bigger-picture win down the road . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I know I can&amp;rsquo ; t change the world at large but I am really lucky to be able to try to create the world I would like to see . I think that&amp;rsquo ; s a privilege not everyone has , &amp;rdquo ; said Jun , adding her focus is how she works hard to create workplaces where she would actually want to work . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m running this stuff . I&amp;rsquo ; m running these businesses . I&amp;rsquo ; m running these things . It is kind of a little bit of a challenge to make it in a way that feels good for me . You know , is it the kind of place where I would want to work even if I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t running it ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jun is just one of the nearly 30 accomplished storytellers and adventurers who will be taking part in the nine days of VIMFF . Founded in 1998 , the festival presents 70 films from B.C. and around the world . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The VIMFF also includes a rich program of clinics , workshops and fun outdoor activity days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;43567;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;43594;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;43628;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;43659;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;43701;TOOLONG ... talent Margaret Cho is unmasked for JFL **30;43736;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;43768;TOOLONG ... latest Metro Vancouver arts **25;43803;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;43830;TOOLONG ... in The Vancouver Sun&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;43866;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? ArtsMoviesSix shorts set to shine in latest edition of Crazy 8 's film challengehttp : **31;44076;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;44109;TOOLONG ... title= " Writer/director Ali Liebert on the set of her new short film The Quieting . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of The Quieting " **28;44145;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1215688 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Crazy8s Gala&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Feb. 22 , 6 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; The Centre in Vancouver &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : //crazy8s.film/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;crazy8s.film &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Longtime actor &lt;a href= " https : **30;44175;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Ali Liebert&lt;/a&gt; has directed two feature-length TV films but it&amp;rsquo ; s her short film debut at this year&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " http : //crazy8s.film " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Crazy8s Gala&lt;/a&gt; that has the Duncan native excited . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The thing that&amp;rsquo ; s really cool is I&amp;rsquo ; ve never seen a film of mine on a big screen . They&amp;rsquo ; ve just been for TV , &amp;rdquo ; said Liebert referring to the films &lt;a href= " https : **31;44207;TOOLONG ? r ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Amish Abduction&lt;/a&gt; and a yet to be released Christmas feature she shot . &amp;ldquo ; So this is really special for me and , you know , my family&amp;rsquo ; s coming over from Vancouver Island and they&amp;rsquo ; ve never seen anything I&amp;rsquo ; ve done on a big screen either . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Liebert &amp;lsquo ; s 13 minute film The Quieting is one of six selected for this the 21st edition of the Crazy8s eight-day filmmaking event . The six films , chosen from 196 applicants , will be screened on Feb. 22 at The Centre in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The other five films are : Itsy Bitsy Spider from director Brodi-Jo Scalise ; Mr. James is Dead directed by Daniel Irving and Josh Aries ; Sol directed by Andy Alvarez ; The Substitute from Malibu Taetz and This is a Period Piece from Bruna Arbex. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of the close to 200 teams that submitted a short video pitch 40 were then chosen to pitch in person to a jury of film industry professionals . After that 12 teams advanced to the script stage where they went through story editing sessions with professional story editors . From there the scripts were debated and championed by the final jury before the six finalists were announced at an event in early January . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is really nothing like Crazy8s . Our entire local film and television industry rallies around the final six films and elevates the production possibilities , &amp;rdquo ; said Erin Mussolum co-executive director/executive producer of the Crazy8s Film Society . &amp;ldquo ; Crazy8s Film Society is a talent incubator . We want to give filmmakers as much experience as possible working with great gear houses , having them align with industry professionals , &amp;nbsp ; providing them union actors , and introducing them to the guilds and industry that are the backbone of our local industry . What can be done in just eight days is really astounding ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The result are films that lean heavily into the themes of &amp;ldquo ; climate change , self identity , psychological stresses and family dynamics , &amp;rdquo ; according to Mussolum. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44240;TOOLONG ... title= " Writer/director Ali Liebert and Sara Canning at work on the new film The Quieting. " alt= " Writer/director Ali Liebert and Sara Canning at work on the new film The Quieting. " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of The Quieting " **28;44276;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1215686 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Liebert&amp;rsquo ; s The Quieting , which she also wrote , fits firmly into the self-identify column as it is based on her own story of coming out in her thirties . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In what Liebert calls a &amp;ldquo ; little time capsule of one night , &amp;rdquo ; the film has the 33-year-old newly queer Maggie ( Sara Canning ) go down a memory lane on the eve of an important date and confront her teenage self ( Julia Stone ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I haven&amp;rsquo ; t seen a lot of stories about people coming out sort of quote unquote later and actually it still feels like a turning point in my life , &amp;rdquo ; said Liebert , 39 , who came out five years ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She hopes that her efforts will resonate with others who are coming out or thinking about doing so . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I hope that it does inspire people to be who they are and come out at any age . It&amp;rsquo ; s not saying that you are not going to have to quiet those voices whether it is the fear of what other people are going to think or just your own internalized homophobia , or fear of judgment , but I think the movie ultimately , I hope , will be really uplifting , &amp;rdquo ; said Liebert , just hours after putting the final lock on the film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Liebert , a Canadian Screen Award winning actor for her role in the Global series Bomb Girls , has plans on adding a regular directing career to her already busy acting life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Yeah , eventually I&amp;rsquo ; d like to be a good enough director that I could direct or co direct myself in something , but I&amp;rsquo ; m not there yet . &amp;ldquo ; Yeah , I&amp;rsquo ; m just trying to figure out how to like , Bradley Cooper it , &amp;rdquo ; said Liebert with a laugh referring to the Star is Born star and director . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s worth noting that Liebert is in talks about a project that fits the above &amp;ldquo ; Bradley Cooper , &amp;rdquo ; bill . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the meantime Liebert is recovering from the crazy Crazy8s schedule and looking forward to a night with colleagues and family at the gala . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think even though the schedule is really crazy , I think it&amp;rsquo ; s pretty incredible that I will have like a completed short film in such a short amount of time . I think that this has helped me , not spend the next six months sort of like meandering , &amp;rdquo ; said Liebert , referring to being left to her own timeline. &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m a person that does better with deadlines , definitely . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;44306;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;44333;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;44367;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeMon , 17 Feb 2020 19:00:18 **45;44398;TOOLONG ... ArtsMoviesHollywood North : Mighty Ducks fly again with Emilio Estevez behind benchhttp : **35;44445;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;44482;TOOLONG ... title= " Emilio Estevez is back as Coach Gordon Bombay for a Mighty Ducks series on Disney Plus . The series will film in Vancouver . " alt= " Emilio Estevez is back as Coach Gordon Bombay for a Mighty Ducks series on Disney Plus . The series will film in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " Disney+/Twitter " **28;44518;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1216974 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gordon Bombay and the Mighty Ducks are flying north to reboot the classic hockey movie franchise from 90s as a TV series for the Disney Plus streaming service . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Emilio Estevez , who played coach Bombay in the three Mighty Ducks films , has signed on to star in the 10-episode comedy which gets rolling next week in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Once a Duck , always a Duck ! &amp;rdquo ; Estevez said in statement . &amp;ldquo ; And after 25 years , I am delighted to lace up my skates , put on Coach Bombay&amp;rsquo ; s jacket and return to play the iconic character for this new chapter in the Mighty Ducks franchise . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gilmore Girls star Lauren Graham will co-star with whole new cast of young Ducks that include Brady Noon ( Good Boys ) , Julee Cerda ( Passengers ) and youngsters Swayam Bhatia ( Succession ) , Taegen Burns ( The Right Stuff ) , Bella Higginbotham ( Troop Zero ) , Luke Islam ( America&amp;rsquo ; s Got Talent ) , Kiefer O&amp;rsquo ; Reilly ( Legends of Tomorrow ) , Maxwell Simkins ( Bizaardvark ) and De&amp;rsquo ; Jon Watts ( Black Panther ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The series is set in present day Minnesota and team has evolved from scrappy underdogs to an ultra-competitive , powerhouse youth hockey team . After 12-year-old Evan ( Noon ) is unceremoniously cut from the Ducks , he and his mom Alex ( Graham ) set out to build their own ragtag team of misfits to challenge the cutthroat , win-at-all-costs culture of competitive youth sports . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is no word if Kenan Thompson and Joshua Jackson , a couple original Ducks , will take part in the series . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Production begins on Monday and will wrap on June 11 . The series is will stream late this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;TONI COLLETTE STARS IN NETFLIX SERIES&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The always brilliant Toni Collette will be in Vancouver next month to star in the eight-episode Netflix series Pieces of Her . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Based on the novel by author Karin Slaughter , Collette plays Laura , a woman with a secret past that she has hidden from her daughter but it all gets exposed when a Saturday afternoon trip to the mall explodes into violence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The series , which will be helmed by veteran TV director Minkie Spiro ( Downton Abbey , Better Call Saul ) , starts filming March . 16. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44548;TOOLONG ... title= " Leslie Mann stars in The Power . " data-portal-copyright= " Rich Polk " **28;45657;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1214076 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Filmmaker , author , actor , comic , podcaster and producer Kevin Smith is returning to his old alma mater. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was while studying at the Vancouver Film School , Smith began work on his breakout indie hit Clerks with VFS alumni and creative collaborators Scott Mosier and Dave Klein . Clerks ( 1994 ) would launch the character of Silent Bob , Smith&amp;rsquo ; s half of the blunted duo of Jay ( played by Jason Mewes ) and Silent Bob . The two characters would subsequently appear in follow-up films such as Chasing Amy , Clerks II and Jay and Silent Bob Reboot. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Smith was quoted in a VFS press release , commenting on the crucial role the institution played in his progress : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Vancouver Film School was the first step on the road to making Clerks . In the hallowed halls of VFS , I not only learned the basics of my craft &amp;mdash ; I also met other likeminded dreamers who would go on to help me tell my stories for the rest of my life . I&amp;rsquo ; m proud and humbled to be named a Creative Ambassador for my alma mater , and I look forward to sharing what little I know with the next generation of VFS artists ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As a VFS creative ambassador , Smith will be making frequent appearances on campus to offer up mentorship and support to students , as well as a Storyteller&amp;rsquo ; s Studio AMA event . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The director , whose films have grossed millions at the global box office , is also behind a new scholarship initiative for aspiring creatives wanting to attend the Vancouver Film School . In 2019 , he awarded over $250,000 in writing , acting and film scholarships at the VFS. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; VFS president James Griffin announced Smith as the first VFS creative ambassador : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It is a remarkable event to have Kevin Smith as our first creative ambassador , &amp;rdquo ; said Griffin . &amp;ldquo ; Kevin attended VFS in the earliest days and has maintained a connection ever since . His success across film , TV , and digital media as writer , director , producer , actor , and celebrity is a testament to his immense talent . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 2018 , Smith played a role in saving the Rio Theatre from closure . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;45687;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;45723;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;45759;TOOLONG " **36;45790;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;45828;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;45857;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynThu , 13 Feb 2020 19:22:18 **45;45888;TOOLONG ... things to see at the 2020 Talking Stick Festivalhttp : **32;45935;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;45969;TOOLONG ... title= " Raven Kanetakta and ShoShona Kish are Digging Roots , playing Feb. 22 , 8:30 p.m. at Roundhouse Exhibition Hall . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of VIFF " **28;47166;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1210524 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anyone who has driven an automobile with a manual stick shift can relate to the rush &amp;mdash ; the pure pop of adrenalin &amp;mdash ; that comes from gearing up or down , especially in a fast-moving vehicle . It&amp;rsquo ; s precisely that feeling , the spine-tingling sensation of cornering before the big straightaway , that defines Ford vs . Ferrari , the best sport movie of 2019. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The relationship between man and machine that finds its visual metaphor in driver and stick shift is referenced time and time again during the two-hour , 32-minute film based on the true story of American race car designer Carroll Shelby and his hand-picked driver , the temperamental Brit Ken Miles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although we&amp;rsquo ; re introduced to the Riverside International Raceway in California and the Daytona Beach International Speedway in Florida , the story is all about the corporate infighting , hierarchical politics , personal demons and irrepressible egos that made the 1966 running of the iconic 24-hour race at Le Mans , France , one of the most notable in auto racing history . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Under the direction of James Mangold , the cinematography of Phedon Papamichael and the razor-sharp film editing of Andrew Buckland , Michael McCusker and Dirk Westervelt , Ford vs . Ferrari takes us inside the Ford GT40 Mk I that finished in a sweep of the top three positions at Le Mans in 1966. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Extreme close ups of the driver Miles &amp;mdash ; shifting back and forth from his sweaty face and riveted eyes to his sure hand on the clutch &amp;mdash ; are used repeatedly to bring us as close as possible to the RPMs and laws of physics that make auto racing a never-ending pursuit of speed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Led by Donald Sylvester , Paul Massey , David Giammarco and Steven Morrow , a sound department of 37 engineers , mixers , boom operators , foley artists and editors do the rest , giving Ford vs . Ferrari the palpable crispness and raw authenticity that has resulted in four nominations at the 92nd Academy Awards on tap Sunday night ( CTV ) at the Dolby Theater in Hollywood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ford vs . Ferrari is up for an Oscar for best picture , best film editing , best sound editing and best sound mixing , making it the most critically-acclaimed and nominated sport movie since The Fighter earned seven Academy Award nominations in 2010 . The epic yet extremely focused First World War movie 1917 will almost certainly follow up its Golden Globe for best drama with the Oscar for best picture , but there&amp;rsquo ; s no question that the audiovisual intensity of Ford vs . Ferrari has a very good chance at being rewarded in one or more of the editing or sound categories . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The movie starring Matt Damon as Shelby and Christian Bale as Miles has scored multiple nominations ( 65 ) and award wins ( 21 ) over the past 60 days , not including eight of 22 categories in The Sport Market Movie Awards for 2019 . By doing so , Ford vs . Ferrari equals the most recent accomplished Formula One racing film Rush &amp;mdash ; the story of the deep-seated rivalry between Austria&amp;rsquo ; s Nikki Lauda and England&amp;rsquo ; s James Hunt &amp;mdash ; which won eight times in The Sport Market Movie Awards in 2013. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both Damon and Bale are solid in their respective roles , but it is the former Batman who is literally in the driver&amp;rsquo ; s seat for much of Ford vs . Ferrari ( billed and marketed as Le Mans &amp;rsquo ; 66 in most parts of Europe ) . Bale was last recognized nine years ago in the sport movie genre for his role as Dicky Eklund , the half-brother of Boston boxer Micky Ward , in The Fighter . He won the Oscar for best supporting actor while Melissa Leo took home best supporting actress as Ward&amp;rsquo ; s mother . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He didn&amp;rsquo ; t receive an Academy Award nomination as best actor for his role as Miles , but Bale&amp;rsquo ; s depiction as the talented mechanic and mercurial driver in Ford vs . Ferrari did earn him best actor nominations at the Golden Globes and Screen Actors Guild Awards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ford vs . Ferrari is this year&amp;rsquo ; s box office champion among sport movies &amp;mdash ; with big screen receipts of US$222 million on the eve of its digital release this Tuesday &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; and wins six other categories of The Sport Market Movie Awards , including best picture , best drama , best cinematography , best editing , best sound and best supporting actress ( Caitriona Balfe ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was clearly the year&amp;rsquo ; s best sport movie &amp;mdash ; and one of the best all-time technical performances in the sport genre in sound and editing &amp;mdash ; in an interesting crop of 42 releases in 2019 ( including a dozen foreign language dramatic productions and 15 sport documentaries ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Netflix original High Flying Bird not only showed us what it would look like if Jerry Maguire ( 1996 ) married Moneyball ( 2011 ) , it demonstrated how streaming companies have completely reshaped the landscapes of movie production , distribution and promotion in recent years . The excellent sport business movie that puts an agent ( Ray Burke played by Andre Holland ) in the middle of the NBA and its players in a lockout is one of several produced this past year by the studio arm at Netflix . It wins three categories in The Sport Market Movie Awards , including best direction by Stephen Soderbergh , best screenplay by Tarell Alvin McCraney and best supporting actor for Melvin Gregg ( who plays Burke&amp;rsquo ; s client Erick Scott ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47196;TOOLONG ... title= " Adam Sandler accepts Best Actor for Uncut Gems onstage during AARP The Magazine 's 19th Annual Movies For Grownups Awards at Beverly Wilshire , A Four Seasons Hotel on January 11 , 2020 in Beverly Hills , California . " alt= " Adam Sandler accepts Best Actor for Uncut Gems onstage during AARP The Magazine 's 19th Annual Movies For Grownups Awards at Beverly Wilshire , A Four Seasons Hotel on January 11 , 2020 in Beverly Hills , California . " data-portal-copyright= " Kevin Winter " **28;47232;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1210535 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , another Netflix production ignored by the Academy this weekend , scores as the best sport-themed movie of the year . It is Uncut Gems , the crime thriller starring Adam Sandler in a compelling dramatic turn as Howard Ratner , a charismatic jeweller who gets himself into trouble in a high-stakes sports betting jam . Kevin Garnett , one of the best power forwards in NBA history , plays himself as Ratner&amp;rsquo ; s client , making his 2008 championship ring a co-star in the flick . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Uncut Gems also features notable performances by newcomer Julia Fox and Idina Menzel as Ratner&amp;rsquo ; s wife Dinah . Menzel is best-known as the speaking and singing voice of Elsa in the animated Disney franchise Frozen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among the other sport movie releases from 2019 that are worth a watch is the year&amp;rsquo ; s best sport documentary &amp;mdash ; Diego Maradona &amp;mdash ; and Fighting With My Family , a biopic dramedy starring Florence Pugh as English WWE wrestler Saraya-Jade Bevis , better known as Paige . Pugh is this year&amp;rsquo ; s best actress in a sport movie and Fighting with My Family , which also features Dwayne Johnson a.k.a . The Rock , wins a total of four categories in The Sport Market Movie Award , adding best biopic and best comedy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Best music , best soundtrack and best song in a sport movie &amp;mdash ; Pray for a Miracle by Romell &amp;mdash ; all come from Brian Banks , the true story of the Long Beach Poly all-American and USC star whose NFL career was put on hold after he was imprisoned for a sexual assault he did not commit . Banks , played by Aldis Hodge , ultimately made his NFL debut for the Atlanta Falcons 10 years later in 2013. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;The Sport Market on TSN Radio rates and debates the bulls and bears of sport business .
