" data-portal-copyright= " Universal Pictures " **28;47644;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204389 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Production designer &lt;a href= " https : **30;47674;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Dennis Gassner&lt;/a&gt; will be wearing a 30-year-old suit when he sits in the &lt;a href= " https : //dolbytheatre.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Dolby Theatre in Los Angeles&lt;/a&gt; on Feb. 9 waiting to see if he and &lt;a href= " https : **30;47706;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Lee Sandales&lt;/a&gt; win the Academy Award for achievement in production design for their work on &lt;a href= " https : **31;47738;TOOLONG ? r ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;best picture nominee 1917&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But that suit isn&amp;rsquo ; t just any old suit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ll wear the same tuxedo I wore when I won for &lt;a href= " https : **31;47771;TOOLONG ? r ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Bugsy&lt;/a&gt; , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner , who , along with &lt;a href= " https : **30;47804;TOOLONG ? re ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Nancy Haigh&lt;/a&gt; , collected the Oscar for best art direction-set decoration in 1991 for the &lt;a href= " https : **30;47836;TOOLONG ? re ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Warren Beatty&lt;/a&gt; -starring film . &amp;ldquo ; It is the only ( tuxedo ) I have ever had and I think the most expensive thing that I ever bought back in 1991 . So , the amortization has been quite nice . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the record , the suit is a Giorgio Armani Classico double-breasted tuxedo. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The style fits in with the movies I make , you know , the period films , &amp;rdquo ; said the 71-year-old Vancouver native , who credits 40 years of yoga as the key to fitting into a suit bought as a much younger man . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47868;TOOLONG ... title= " Production designer Dennis Gassner is nominated for his seventh Academy Award for his work on the film 1917 . Photo : Kevin Winter/Getty Images " alt= " Production designer Dennis Gassner is nominated for his seventh Academy Award for his work on the film 1917 . Photo : Kevin Winter/Getty Images " data-portal-copyright= " Kevin Winter " **28;47904;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204375 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; This year , the period film that has brought him to the big show with his seventh Academy Award nomination ( his last nomination was 2018&amp;rsquo ; s Blade Runner 2049 ) is the epic First World War drama from Sam Mendes about a pair of young soldiers ( Dean-Charles Chapman and George MacKay ) who are sent out on a mission to try to deliver a message to stop an attack that would result in huge loss of life for the English army . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The best-picture favourite and recent BAFTA sweeper , including production design for Gassner and Sandales , is a visual masterpiece that looks like it&amp;rsquo ; s one long continuous shot . It isn&amp;rsquo ; t . It&amp;rsquo ; s a series of shots that are meticulously put together to give the overwhelming impression that it is one shot and that you , the viewer , are right there beside the scared young men as they make this perilous journey through the French battlegrounds. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This marks the fifth time Gassner has worked with Mendes , and the ninth film he has done with master cinematographer Roger Deakins. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It took over a year to complete 1917 . Each scene and shot was put together like an intricate dance with the utmost in complicated choreography . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You can only do that when you have a lot of experience and especially with the camaraderie that Sam and Roger and I have . Old relationships , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &amp;ldquo ; It was kind of an inch-by-inch journey . Defining it , measuring it , discussing it , and designing it and building it and shooting it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47934;TOOLONG ... title= " Schofield , George MacKay , foreground , with fellow soldiers in 1917 , the new epic from Oscar-winning filmmaker Sam Mendes . " alt= " Schofield , George MacKay , foreground , with fellow soldiers in 1917 , the new epic from Oscar-winning filmmaker Sam Mendes . " data-portal-copyright= " Photo Credit : Fran ? ois Duhamel " **28;47970;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1204386 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for his actual design , Gassner said he had a sense of what he wanted to create as soon as he finished reading Mendes&amp;rsquo ; s script . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I really wanted to have a dreamscape environment or a slightly surreal one , because that kind of goes to the context of what those boys must have gone through , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &amp;ldquo ; The surreal element of war . It certainly was not their normal lives . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Everything you see in this movie is real and no detail goes unnoticed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Everything you saw was created by artists . So , I used the term the art of war . It is going to be an approach that everything is going to be exact , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner about his brief to those working with him on the design of the film . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s going to be perfect in the way that it would have been done as if we were doing it for real . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Every person who worked on this film , I talked to them about that . That was their edict : to make sure that everything they did was precise . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each step and movement was laid out before shooting . There was not an inch of the film that wasn&amp;rsquo ; t measured , marked and considered to the highest degree . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Beat for beat was laid out . It was a very complicated thing to do , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &amp;ldquo ; It was an immaculate dance . In my opinion , perfection , because it had to be . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **33;48000;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;48035;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This was easily one of the more demanding movies Gassner has worked on in his five decades in the business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The last shooting day was the scene where George jumps into the river from the bridge . Sam , Roger and me , we all looked at each other and I said , &amp;lsquo ; I think I&amp;rsquo ; m quite tired . &amp;rsquo ; We all started laughing , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &amp;ldquo ; It is amazing you get through an experience like this and you can smile at it and give everybody a hug and say , &amp;lsquo ; Well done . &amp;rsquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s like running a super-marathon . You go through the finish line and you fall over and you hug each other . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gassner was born in Vancouver in 1948 and lived here for five years before his parents took him and his brother to Portland , Ore . He was raised there and went to the University of Oregon at Eugene , where he was studying architecture . It&amp;rsquo ; s during that time that he saw David Lean&amp;rsquo ; s 1962 cinematic masterpiece Lawrence of Arabia and everything changed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I said , &amp;lsquo ; Now that&amp;rsquo ; s big . That&amp;rsquo ; s big and I want to do big . That is bigger than any building I could ever design and build , &amp;rsquo ; so it was about having a big imagination , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner , who moved to Los Angeles at age 21 and enrolled in the ArtCenter College of Design . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was while at the Pasadena , Calif. , college that he and a pal decided they were going to get a job working for Francis Ford Coppola on Apocalypse Now . And the rest , as they say , is history , as Gassner did go on to design the movie poster for the legendary Coppola film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Always in demand , Gassner took the 1917 job over another James Bond film &amp;mdash ; he has done three already , two with Mendes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was the unknown , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner , when asked about that decision to go to war with Mendes . &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know that much about the Great War and I wanted to explore , and hopefully tell the truest of the stories . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The story for 1917 came from Mendes&amp;rsquo ; s memories of the stories his grandfather , a First World War veteran , told him as a kid . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was the most powerful script I have ever read , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And Gassner has seen some pretty great scripts . A glance at his resum&amp;eacute ; and you can&amp;rsquo ; t help but utter &amp;ldquo ; wow . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s been a nice journey , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner , a two-time BAFTA winner ( Road to Perdition and The Truman Show ) , when asked about his IMDb page listings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; More like epic voyage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He has done six Cohen Brothers films and worked with the likes of Barry Levinson , Tim Burton , Mark Forster , Denis Villeneuve , Rob Marshall and Chris Weitz . And he is part of one of cinema&amp;rsquo ; s most storied franchises &amp;mdash ; Bond , James Bond . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was exciting , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner about the Bond films ( Spectre , Skyfall and Quantum of Solace ) . &amp;ldquo ; There is a lot to do because there is a huge history &amp;hellip ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a huge , huge responsibility and something I have been lucky to do three times , and I will probably do again later on and down the line . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fun fact : The single shot , opening Day of the Dead scene in Spectre , directed by Mendes , is where the inspiration for 1917&amp;rsquo ; s one-shot look came from . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s where we got the idea to do 1917 , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &amp;ldquo ; We figured out how to do it and it was actually pretty cool . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When Gassner is asked about the amazing list of talented filmmakers he has worked with , he pauses and says , &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s always fun to be with friends . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; OK , but is there something else besides a good time with old pals that connects the dots between the projects of his impressive career ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Good storytellers . Everything is about story . That is all I care about , is story , &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With that statement in support of story , it seems only fitting to end this piece with a great story . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It was 2002 , and Gassner was at the Governor&amp;rsquo ; s Ball after the Academy Awards . While winding his way through the tables to the bathroom , someone slid their chair back into his path . There , in front of Gassner , was the legendary actor Peter O&amp;rsquo ; Toole , Lawrence in Lawrence of Arabia . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; O&amp;rsquo ; Toole had been f&amp;ecirc ; ted earlier in the evening with a lifetime achievement award . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I said , &amp;lsquo ; Oh , Mr. O&amp;rsquo ; Toole , I would like to introduce myself . My name is Dennis Gassner . I&amp;rsquo ; m here because I&amp;rsquo ; m nominated for a film , and congratulations on your lifetime achievement award . &amp;rsquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And then I said , &amp;lsquo ; I am here today because of you and what you did on Lawrence of Arabia , &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; said Gassner. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He looked at me and bowed and said , &amp;lsquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m honoured . &amp;rsquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then he said , &amp;lsquo ; Where are you going ? &amp;rsquo ; I said , &amp;lsquo ; To the loo , &amp;rsquo ; and he said , &amp;lsquo ; Yeah , me too . Let&amp;rsquo ; s go . &amp;rsquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So there I was standing next to Peter O&amp;rsquo ; Toole at a urinal and I looked over and said , &amp;lsquo ; Hey , Peter , when you were in the desert , where did you pee ? &amp;rsquo ; and he said , &amp;lsquo ; Anywhere I wanted . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And , cut . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;48071;TOOLONG " **32;48098;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;48132;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? NewsMoviesHollywood North : Sandra Bullock filming Netflix feature in Vancouverhttp : **31;48528;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;48561;TOOLONG ... title= " Sandra Bullock is shooting a movie for Netflix in Vancouver . " alt= " Sandra Bullock is shooting a movie for Netflix in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " Theo Wargo/NBC " **28;48597;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1205938 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cameras are set to roll this week in Vancouver on a still untitled Netflix feature starring Sandra Bullock. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bullock , who scored a hit on the streaming service last year with the post-apocalyptic supernatural thriller Bird Box , plays a woman re-entering society after serving a lengthy prison sentence for a violent crime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The movie , which also stars Viola Davis , is being helmed by German director Nora Fingscheidt and was written by Christopher McQuarrie , who penned the Usual Suspects and a slew of Tom Cruise projects . He wrote and directed the last two Mission Impossible films . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vincent D&amp;rsquo ; Onofrio ( Full Metal Jacket , Daredevil ) , Jon Bernthal ( The Walking Dead , Punisher ) and Richard Thomas ( The Waltons ) are also cast in the movie , which according to the Directors Guild of Canada production list , was scheduled to start production Monday and wrap on April 9. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48627;TOOLONG ... title= " Sandra Bullock is filming a Netflix movie in Vancouver . " alt= " Sandra Bullock is filming a Netflix movie in Vancouver . " data-portal-copyright= " LTA " **28;48663;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1205939 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;VANCOUVER BECOMES NEVERLAND&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Disney Plus , the house of mouse&amp;rsquo ; s streaming service , will also be shooting a big budget feature in Vancouver this spring as director David Lowery comes to town to make a live-action Peter Pan movie . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film , tentatively titled Peter Pan and Wendy , is scheduled to start filming April 13 under the production title Bonfire . It&amp;rsquo ; s expected to wrap up mid-August . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We don&amp;rsquo ; t know who will play Peter or Wendy , or whether there will even be a Tinkerbell , because casting has not yet been announced . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This will be Lowery&amp;rsquo ; s second live-action remake of a Disney classic . In 2016 , he brought a re-booted Pete&amp;rsquo ; s Dragon to the big screen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;PILOT SEASON&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s film and television community is gearing for TV pilot season which gets underway next month . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The were 10 pilots in Vancouver last year , more than any other North American production hub , and so far , we know of two NBC pilots that will be filmed here next month &amp;mdash ; the sinkhole drama La Brea and the science-fiction thriller Debris . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; La Brea tells the story of a family trying to survive after they are separated by a massive sinkhole that mysteriously opens in Los Angeles , while Debris is an X-Files-like show about agents investigating an alien spacecraft that is causing mysterious effects on people . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Production on Debris starts March 3 , while filming on La Brea begins March 10. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;TWILIGHT ZONE TRAVELS TO INTERIOR&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Twilight Zone&amp;rsquo ; s Vancouver crew packed up and headed east Monday for a week-long location shoot in Ashcroft . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nearly 200 cast and crew have descended on the Interior B.C. village , which has a population of 1,558 . Sources say the Twilight Zone crew has booked every hotel room in Ashcroft and nearby Cache Creek , along with some spillover in Kamloops. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Actor Craig Robinson , who played Darryl on The Office , is rumoured to star in the episode which is titled A Small Town . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Filmmaker Jordan Peele&amp;rsquo ; s reboot of the classic Rod Serling series airs on CBS All Access in the U.S. and CITY-TV in Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;48693;TOOLONG " **34;48722;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **26;48758;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;48786;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; Scott BrownTue , 04 Feb 2020 18:19:59 **45;48820;TOOLONG ... **25;48867;TOOLONG Minute : The Gentlemen reviewhttp : **34;48894;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;48930;TOOLONG ... title= " Colin Farrell and Charlie Hunnam in The Gentlemen . " alt= " Colin Farrell and Charlie Hunnam in The Gentlemen . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of VVS " **28;48966;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1196457 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;48996;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Filmmaker Guy Ritchie is back with a rough , tough tale of an American ex-pat drug lord in London ( Matthew McConaughey ) who wants to get out of the game but sees the sale of his business go super sideways as gangster after gangster gets involved . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Joining McConaughey in this foul-mouthed ( duh , it&amp;rsquo ; s Ritchie ) crime comedy is Charlie Hunnam , Michelle Dockery , Colin Farrell , Henry Golding and Hugh Grant in an absolutely fantastic turn . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To find out if you&amp;rsquo ; re the type of punter who would enjoy this f&amp;rsquo ; ing movie , check out this week&amp;rsquo ; s Movie Minute with Postmedia film critic Dana Gee and Arts &amp;amp ; Life editor Aleesha Harris . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeWed , 22 Jan 2020 22:50:50 **45;49032;TOOLONG ... Short Film Festival festival long on local talenthttp : **34;49079;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;49115;TOOLONG ... title= " Lillian Lim , left , and Wonita Joy play mother and daughter respectively in the short film Grandma 's 80th Surprise , part of the 10th annual Vancouver Short Film Festival on Jan. 24-26 , 2020. " alt= " Lillian Lim , left , and Wonita Joy play mother and daughter respectively in the short film Grandma 's 80th Surprise , part of the 10th annual Vancouver Short Film Festival on Jan. 24-26 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Photo credit : Handout " **28;49151;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1190191 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vancouver Short Film Festival&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Jan. 24-26 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Vancity Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : //www.vsff.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;vsff.com&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; After watching the film The Farewell , &lt;a href= " https : **30;49181;TOOLONG ? re ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Curtis Lum&lt;/a&gt; knew he , too , had a family story he wanted to tell . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While director/writer &lt;a href= " https : **30;49213;TOOLONG ? re ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Lulu Wang&lt;/a&gt; &amp;lsquo ; s The Farewell is about a Chinese clan that keeps a big secret from the matriarch , Lum&amp;rsquo ; s film Grandma&amp;rsquo ; s 80th Surprise is a story about a Chinese family sharing information with the matriarch. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film , which Lum co-wrote and co-directed with his girlfriend &lt;a href= " https : **30;49245;TOOLONG ? re ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Fola **25;49277;TOOLONG , will be part of this year&amp;rsquo ; s &lt;a href= " http : //vsff.com " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver Short Film Festival&lt;/a&gt; . VSFF is a diverse offering of 46 films that do not get lumped into themes , leaving the event as a sort of cinematic tasting menu that is sure to satisfy curious film lovers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;49304;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;49340;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Lum&amp;rsquo ; s 10-minute long film tells the true tale of Curtis ( Lum ) introducing Ella ( Myrasol Martinez ) to his grandmother for the first time on the occasion of the grandmother&amp;rsquo ; s 80th birthday lunch . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Right away from there I knew I wanted to do a film to honour my grandmother , and to share her with the world , but I also wanted to do a film that would make everyone want to immediately call up their mothers or grandmothers and want to give them a big ol&amp;rsquo ; hug , &amp;rdquo ; said Lum , who splits his time between Vancouver and Los Angeles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Interestingly , though at first the grandmother as drawn by the Curtis character gives you pause , it becomes clear that Curtis&amp;rsquo ; s concern is how his grandmother will react when she meets Ella and sees that she is black . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the film Curtis is a nervous wreck who has gone so far as to enlist his mother to talk to the grandmother and break the black ice before they break bread . Other family members fuel Curtis&amp;rsquo ; s anxiety and cause Ella to question what she is about to face . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t say she wasn&amp;rsquo ; t racist . I said she wasn&amp;rsquo ; t MAGA racist , &amp;rdquo ; a jittery Curtis says to Ella. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;49376;TOOLONG ... title= " Curtis Lum is the star and co-writer and co-director of the entertaining short film Grandma 's 80th Surprise . " alt= " Curtis Lum is the star and co-writer and co-director of the entertaining short film Grandma 's 80th Surprise . " data-portal-copyright= " Handout " **28;49412;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1188149 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like a birthday cake , you can cut the tension and angst in this story with a knife but , thankfully , there is a very healthy resolve &amp;mdash ; a resolve that actually occurred between Lum and his grandmother . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; All this time I thought my grandmother was racist , but in actuality it was all just one big miscommunication , &amp;rdquo ; said Lum. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This story , for me , was all about celebrating the miscommunication we have inter-generationally , and breaking out of our rigid culture and family tradition and celebrating a breakthrough I had with my grandmother , &amp;rdquo ; added Lum , who is a very busy actor and is also developing projects for his Arrival of the Fittest Entertainment production company . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Her and I now have a deeper , stronger relationship . I feel like we can talk about anything &amp;hellip ; Now she is so much more appreciative that I can be open and honest with her and vice versa , which is really nice . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;49442;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;49484;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;49520;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;49551;TOOLONG ... North : The latest from the B.C. TV and film **29;49587;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;49618;TOOLONG ... latest Metro Vancouver arts **25;49654;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the film , the grandmother is played by Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Lillian Lim . Lim ( Meditation Park ) , who said she didn&amp;rsquo ; t have to go far for inspiration for her character as her own mother was raised with cultural racism . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My mom was really like that , &amp;rdquo ; said Lim , adding that she is the middle child with a brother on either side . &amp;ldquo ; She told all three of us when we were growing up that &amp;lsquo ; if you marry anybody that is not Chinese , I will disown you . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lim , though , reports her mother softened that view as Lim grew older . In fact when she was 29 her mother told it was okay to widen her marriage net . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; At the time , to me , it was an insult . That I was in a desperate place , desperate to be married , &amp;rdquo ; said Lim with a big laugh . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When Lum ( who was also in Meditation Park ) asked Lim to do his film , Lim was excited but had one request &amp;mdash ; she wanted her character to speak Toisan ( Toisanese ) , a country village dialect spoken by her own mother . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That was one of the wishes on my bucket list that I would do a film in Toisan , &amp;rdquo ; said Lim , &amp;nbsp ; who is currently working on a Disney + TV series . &amp;ldquo ; So my wish came true . It&amp;rsquo ; s kind of a dying language . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At its heart , Grandma&amp;rsquo ; s 80th Surprise is a story about love conquering all but that message is delivered through the lens of cultural biases and racism . Topics that are still very fresh today . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Right now is the perfect time for a story like this , &amp;rdquo ; said Lum , who initially did the film as part of the Mighty Asian Movie Marathon competition . &amp;ldquo ; We still need to talk about these things . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lum&amp;rsquo ; s instincts were bang-on , as festival audiences of all cultures and backgrounds have embraced the sweet little movie . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was floored and overjoyed by the reaction we have got , &amp;rdquo ; said Lum about the film that premiered at the Vancouver Asian Film Festival in November . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We were in front of a predominantly Asian audience so I expected it to go over well , but surprisingly a lot of the people that have come up to us , that have stayed around after the screening to talk to us , a lot of them that it resonated with , weren&amp;rsquo ; t even Asian at all . That was what was the most beautiful for me as far as takeaway. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film , that has already earned awards at a handful of other festivals , is part of the exciting VSFF 10th anniversary program , a program that has been growing steadily since the festival started . This year organizers had 265 submissions to pick from . That number has doubled from four years ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s kind of crazy we are selling out screenings , we have rush lines to every program , &amp;rdquo ; said VSFF co-director Marena Dix. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think a lot of people are creating more in the city , and there has been a lot more opportunities for that . We&amp;rsquo ; re just really happy to have a place we can showcase what Vancouver independent talent has to offer . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;49681;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;49723;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;49759;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;49790;TOOLONG ... North : The latest from the B.C. TV and film **29;49826;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;49857;TOOLONG ... latest Metro Vancouver arts **25;49893;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Short films are getting a lot more attention thanks to festivals like this , and streaming services and YouTube and Vimeo. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Our festival is a great place to show what you want to do as a filmmaker and where you want to go as a creator , &amp;rdquo ; said Dix , who works in film and TV post production . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a melting pot of ideas . It&amp;rsquo ; s so fun . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those shorts can then become great calling cards for a filmmaker. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For me , an incredible opportunity to play and learn , &amp;rdquo ; said Lum who has produced two other short films . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; As a producer , what I love about tackling a good short film is that it is easy to get eyeballs on the film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; To me , a short film is a great opportunity for people to have a chance to watch it and digest it , &amp;rdquo ; added Lum. &amp;ldquo ; People don&amp;rsquo ; t always have 100 minutes to watch a movie . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The short film is also an important part of building a career and continued professional growth for actors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I love doing them , &amp;rdquo ; said Lim , who came to acting later in life . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For all the other stuff you usually will never get a lead role , never . You&amp;rsquo ; re almost always background when you book anything . But for the short movies you get the lead role or the supporting lead role and , that&amp;rsquo ; s the only way you can showcase yourself as an actor , what you are capable of doing . That&amp;rsquo ; s why I love doing short movies . It&amp;rsquo ; s like a free acting class . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This year to celebrate the 10th anniversary of the VSFF , the program has been expanded from two to three days . It will open with a special screening of the best picture winners from the last five years of the festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;49920;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;49962;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;50792;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1190137 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; DOLCE E GIALLI : Founding co-curators Tom Charity and Giulio Recchioni kicked off the seventh annual Italian Film Fest in the Vancity Theatre recently . The Italian Cultural Centre , the Vancouver International Film Festival and the Consulate General of Italy co-presented the weeklong program . Five-month Consul General Fabio Messineo attended the opening event . He returned later to introduce and discuss director Marco Bellocchio&amp;rsquo ; s Il Traditore ( The Traitor ) that was shot in his home island , Sicily . Audiences appreciate the festival&amp;rsquo ; s mix of new and old films , said Charity . The old included two screenings of La Dolce Vita by the late Federico Fellini who would be 100 on Jan. 20 . Recchioni welcomed the festival&amp;rsquo ; s new three-film component , Gialli ( Yellow ) , &amp;ldquo ; that is the Italian version of Noir with more sex , &amp;rdquo ; he said . First-nighters thanked Museum of Vancouver CEO Mauro Vescera , who founded the festival when he was the cultural centre&amp;rsquo ; s executive director . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50822;TOOLONG ... title= " With his 15th album , Day By Day , imminent , jazz saxophonist Cory Weeds joined keyboardist Sharon Minemoto to entertain Italian Film Festival guests . " alt= " With his 15th album , Day By Day , imminent , jazz saxophonist Cory Weeds joined keyboardist Sharon Minemoto to entertain Italian Film Festival guests . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;50858;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1190138 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; PRO&amp;rsquo ; S CONN : As usual , feted city jazzer Cory Weeds entertained Italian Film Festival first-night guests , backed by keyboardist Sharon Minemoto . Weeds&amp;rsquo ; much-vaunted Conn10M tenor sax looked and sounded as fresh as when new almost 80 years ago . Called the Naked Lady because of an engraving on its bell , the Indiana-made sax would be ideal to accompany a festival screening of Roberto Roberti&amp;rsquo ; s 1922 silent film , La Donna Nuda. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50888;TOOLONG ... title= " Late Haida carver , goldsmith and writer-broadcaster Bill Reid , who would have been 100 on Jan. 12 will spur many commemorative events this year . " alt= " Late Haida carver , goldsmith and writer-broadcaster Bill Reid , who would have been 100 on Jan. 12 will spur many commemorative events this year . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;50924;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1190141 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BETTER BY ALF : Like Federico Fellini , Victoria-born Haida artist Bill Reid would have been 100 this month . The Bill Reid Gallery of Northwest Coast Art will stage year-long commemorative events . Master carver , goldsmith and writer-broadcaster Reid was also a dry humorist . At a retirement ceremony for Canada&amp;rsquo ; s first Indigenous lawyer-judge , Alf Scow , Reid presented an artwork depicting his own wolf clan&amp;rsquo ; s symbology. &amp;ldquo ; I created this drawing at great expense and long labour , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; I began it about 3:30 this afternoon . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50954;TOOLONG ... title= " Here with wife Joan , late lawyer-judge Alf Scow jokingly apologized to brother Rupert for breaking his promise " to put all the white men in jail . " " alt= " Here with wife Joan , late lawyer-judge Alf Scow jokingly apologized to brother Rupert for breaking his promise " to put all the white men in jail . " " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;50990;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1190142 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Scow topped that by smilingly telling his largely non-Indigenous well-wishers : &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve apologized to my brother Rupert for not keeping a promise to put all the white men in jail . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51020;TOOLONG ... title= " Brewmaster Kerry Dixon and Tap and Barrel founder/CEO Daniel Frankel brandished B.C. Beer Awards trophies for Brewhall beverages they canned . " alt= " Brewmaster Kerry Dixon and Tap and Barrel founder/CEO Daniel Frankel brandished B.C. Beer Awards trophies for Brewhall beverages they canned . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;51056;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1190139 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; CAN IT : Brewhall Beer Co. owner Daniel Frankel and brewmaster Kerry Dyson did just that after their K&amp;ouml ; l Story Bro K&amp;ouml ; lsch won the 2019 B.C. Beer Awards&amp;rsquo ; Pale German Beer category and Azedo Tropical&amp;nbsp ; Fruit Sour took the People&amp;rsquo ; s Choice Award . Those beverages and others , including customer-favourite Neon Lights Pale Ale , went into tins for the first time recently at the 1918-built Second-off-Quebec facility . That once-derelict building was dismantled , renovated and reopened in 2014 as Steel Toad Brewing . Frankel acquired it in 2017 . With an outlet of his Tap and Barrel chain two blocks away , he needed another name &amp;mdash ; ergo Brewhall &amp;mdash ; for the pub-restaurant and what he calls &amp;ldquo ; an experimental , not a production brewery . &amp;rdquo ; Born like Kiss bassist-singer Gene Simmons in Haifa , Israel , guitarist Frankel also played in a heavy-metal band , The Sabras . Titled 3069 : A Space Sex Odyssey , the album about needing to insure the future of humankind by impregnating the universal Goddess with rock is out on Royal Mountain Records . To say this recording is a far cry from other label acts such as Orville Peck , Alvvays or Calpurnia is understatement . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But as Deaner explained on the phone from Montreal , Nightseeker has always been about standing out from the crowd . He took time to talk to Postmedia about the band and its coming tour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Following the fires in Fort McMurray and the loss of your home , instrument and more that was portrayed in FUBAR Age of Computer , it looks like you ca n't keep a good Deaner down ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **35;51693;TOOLONG : When your bass gets burned up , it might be seen as a sign to stop . That&amp;rsquo ; s what it&amp;rsquo ; s all about . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : 3069 sounds like a lot of classic northern European concept albums from the ' 80s . Was it hard to complete ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;51730;TOOLONG : I like that you used the word concept because it&amp;rsquo ; s like a story , right . What was your musical development ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;51866;TOOLONG : I began in a band called Quicksand , but that name was taken so we became Quakesand , and things were going pretty good . They did , which is good . Then my band in Calgary got hit by lightning and I lost the whole band that night , too , but I was OK . Something about having no testicles seems to make you immune from all those damages , I guess . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What can fans hope to hear in the set ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **30;51898;TOOLONG : Some old classics , for sure . Mostly , it&amp;rsquo ; s the new album because that&amp;rsquo ; s what everyone wants to hear us play . Most of the crowd is always super wasted , so we usually play about an hour to an hour and a half and then keep give&amp;rsquo ; n it with the fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;51930;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **36;51961;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;51999;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;52028;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;52059;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **31;52101;TOOLONG " &g; ... NewsMoviesAussie expats plan Mad Max marathon for wildfire relief at Vancouver 's Rio Theatrehttp : **33;52615;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;52650;TOOLONG ... title= " A scene from Mad Max : Fury Road . The Rio Theatre is hosting a marathon to raise funds for the Australia wildfire relief effort . " alt= " A scene from Mad Max : Fury Road . The Rio Theatre is hosting a marathon to raise funds for the Australia wildfire relief effort . " data-portal-copyright= " Jasin Boland " **28;52686;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1185822 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A group of Aussie expats are throwing a Mad Max marathon at the &lt;a href= " http : //riotheatre.ca/ " &gt;Rio Theatre&lt;/a&gt; to help fight the fires back home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The day-long event at east Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s historic indie cinema , planned for later this month , will screen all four films in George Miller&amp;rsquo ; s beloved and bizarre action franchise , in addition to other entertainment , raffles and special guest appearances . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The benefit , which aims to raise $20,000 to send Down Under , is organized by actors Rhona Rees and Pauline Egan and event planner Tee Haynes &amp;mdash ; three Australians living and working in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Although most of my friends and family are safe and out of the direct line of fire , so to speak , it&amp;rsquo ; s a strange sense of despair and helplessness that comes with being an ex-pat and reading about your home country from afar , &amp;rdquo ; said Rees . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s really strange . So I decided to try and direct that hopeless energy into something positive and made some inquiries at the Rio Theatre about putting on a movie marathon . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It turned out she wasn&amp;rsquo ; t alone . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; To my delight , a gentleman named Tee Haynes had already reached out to get the ball rolling , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; We have joined forces . We&amp;rsquo ; ve got a little army of Aussie expats helping us , running around and getting donations and spreading the word . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Haynes was a step ahead . He had already arranged with Warner Bros. to get the movies at a discounted price , allowing the Rio to raise funds through ticket sales rather than simply trying to recoup screening costs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52716;TOOLONG ... title= " A still from Mad Max : Fury Road . " alt= " A still from Mad Max : Fury Road . " data-portal-copyright= " Submitted " **28;52752;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1185927 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rees and Egan , meanwhile , are drawing on their film-industry connections to plan some fun surprises for guests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve got my people back in Oz , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve got some of the original cast members of Mad Max sending us video messages , which is going to be really fun . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Why Mad Max ? The dystopian franchise is thoroughly Australian , with each of the four films written and directed by Australian George Miller and filmed around the continent . There&amp;rsquo ; s no denying its appeal &amp;mdash ; the most recent entry was one of the best movies of the last decade &amp;mdash ; and there&amp;rsquo ; s no denying the sudden relevance of its bleak , arid , post-apocalyptic vision . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; A lot of people love Mad Max , &amp;rdquo ; said Rees . &amp;ldquo ; And in a kind of sad way , it echoes where we could be going with the climate change and the scale of devastation that these fires are causing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Australia is a land that is used to fire . We have entire species of trees that need fire in order to regenerate , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Usually when you read the news it&amp;rsquo ; s that a bushfire will be contained when it reaches this road , or once it reaches a mountain ridge , it&amp;rsquo ; ll stop . But these fires can not be stopped and it is just so terrifying . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If they can&amp;rsquo ; t fight the fires , Rees explained , they can at least fight the homesickness and helplessness Australians in Vancouver are feeling as they watch the devastation from afar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re planning a really big , fun , high-energy day , because , again , when these disasters happen , the tendency is to despair , &amp;rdquo ; said Rees . &amp;ldquo ; So if we can raise funds , not just by clicking on a link and giving our credit card but by bringing people together and celebrating , and doing something positive and fun , then we&amp;rsquo ; re succeeding , in my opinion . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Mad Max-athon for Aussie Bushfire Relief goes Saturday , Jan. 25 , at the Rio Theatre . &lt;a href= " https : **33;52782;TOOLONG ... are available here&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;52817;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **35;52847;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **28;52884;TOOLONG " class= " twitter-follow-button " data-show-count= " false " &gt;Follow **25;52914;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;52941;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? " data-portal-copyright= " Photo Credit : Fran ? ois Duhamel " **28;53156;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1184070 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;53186;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Director Sam Mendes&amp;rsquo ; s new epic war movie 1917 follows two British soldiers during the First World War as they embark on an impossible mission deep into enemy territory . The film , starring Dean-Charles Chapman and George MacKay , hits theatres this week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Find out what Postmedia film critic Dana Gee thinks of the film in this week&amp;rsquo ; s Movie Minute video with Gee and Postmedia Arts &amp;amp ; Life editor Aleesha Harris . &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeThu , 09 Jan 2020 22:18:45 **45;53222;TOOLONG ... and their watchers stars of documentary shot on the North Coast of B.C.http : **36;53269;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;53307;TOOLONG ... title= " Janie Wray , lead researcher , B.C. Whales , and co-founder of the Cetacea Lab , is seen here observing humpback whales on the North Coast of B.C. " data-portal-copyright= " National Film Board " **28;53343;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1173938 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;53373;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Whale and the **30;53408;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Jan. 10-16 , 2020 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //viff.org " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancity Theatre&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;53440;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Director &lt;a href= " https : **30;53475;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; Mirjam Leuze&lt;/a&gt; &amp;rsquo ; s new documentary &lt;a href= " https : **33;53507;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;The Whale and the Raven&lt;/a&gt; is a cinematically calm , beautiful movie that speaks volumes about the risks whales on B.C.&amp;rsquo ; s North Coast face from a growing gas industry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the heart of the German/NFB co-produced feature-length film is the work being done by researchers Janie Wray of the Fin Island-based &lt;a href= " https : //www.bcwhales.org/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;B.C . Whales&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href= " https : **30;53542;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Cetacea Lab at Whale Point&lt;/a&gt; ( Gil Island ) head researcher Hermann Meuter. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film lands at &lt;a href= " https : **33;53574;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancity Theatre&lt;/a&gt; Jan. 10-16 , 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wanted to get out the beauty of this place and the fascination . And I wanted to get it out in a very sensual way , &amp;rdquo ; Leuze said recently over the phone from Germany . &amp;ldquo ; I wanted people to feel this place . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;53609;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;53645;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Leuze , herself , had a &amp;ldquo ; feel this place&amp;rdquo ; experience that reassured her this film at this time was the right move . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was sitting on the rocks of Fin Island and hearing and feeling in my body the vocalizations of humpbacks . This was a moment that I knew this was such a special place and there were not many places in the world where you can experience something like this , &amp;rdquo ; said Leuze. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That was the moment that I thought &amp;lsquo ; wow this is so magical , so special . &amp;rsquo ; This is great to bring this film and this place out into the world . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By doing so Leuze hopes to remind the public that the No. 1 concern for the North Coast ocean ecosystem , especially in and around the Kitimat fiord system , is the planned increase of liquefied natural gas ( LNG ) tanker traffic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This type of battle is not new for the area as back in 2006 it was selected to be a route for the Enbridge Northern Gateway Pipeline project &amp;mdash ; a proposed pipeline and oil tanker project that would ship Alberta oilsands via Kitimat . That project was stopped but soon after LNG showed up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Despite protests the tanker traffic is green lit and , according to Wray , will begin in late 2023 or early 2024. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Noise pollution is huge , &amp;rdquo ; said Wray who has been working in the area for close to 20 years . &amp;ldquo ; It can interfere with them finding their prey , which is huge . There&amp;rsquo ; s communicating with each other . But the other thing especially when you are talking up north and you&amp;rsquo ; re talking about humpback whales and fin whales it is also vessel strikes . That&amp;rsquo ; s a huge , huge issue . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53681;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53717;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Humpback whales seen here off of Gil Island on the North Coast of B. C. As mentioned , this is a quiet film that manages to draw the viewer into the environment . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I really felt that she captured something different . She captured the connection between people and place , which I think is very important and she just caught those silent moments with the humpbacks , &amp;rdquo ; said Wray , who spends about eight months out of the year in the field . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I mean , look at the first scene where you come in with the drone and there is a humpback sleeping which is a very unusual thing for people to see . I don&amp;rsquo ; t think they realize that up there these humpbacks actually float on the surface &amp;mdash ; which puts them at risk . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When you are standing on land and these large beings are sleeping ( they do so anywhere from five minutes to two hours at a time ) just metres away from you , well , it&amp;rsquo ; s just such a unique experience and you feel connected in a different way , &amp;rdquo ; added Wray who with Meuter long ago received permission from First Nations to work in their territory and have since included members of the Gitga&amp;rsquo ; at in the research process . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53747;TOOLONG ... title= " Janie Wray , lead researcher , B.C. Whales , and co-founder of the Cetacea Lab on a whale research excursion in the waters off the west coast of British Columbia . " alt= " Janie Wray , lead researcher , B.C. Whales , and co-founder of the Cetacea Lab on a whale research excursion in the waters off the west coast of British Columbia . " data-portal-copyright= " National Film Board of Canada " **28;53783;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1173939 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film , shot over six months from spring to late fall in 2017 , focuses much of its time on the remote Gil Island and Fin Island . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was challenging . Especially in autumn when the storms were coming in , &amp;rdquo ; said Leuze who calls Cologne Germany home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Leuze came to tell this story through what she calls a couple of coincidences . First , when she was a teenager in the early 1980s , she visited an uncle who had moved to Kitimat decades earlier . A further connection to the North Coast came when it turned out her husband Cyrill Tjoa&amp;rsquo ; s university friend and fellow whale enthusiast was Meuter. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Both of them went for a world tour for one year following the whales , &amp;rdquo ; said Leuze. &amp;ldquo ; Hermann happened to fall in love with orcas on the West Coast of British Columbia . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The rest they say is history as Meuter and Wray , who were together at the time , went on to found the Cetacea Lab. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We helped Hermann and Janie build their own structure in 2003 . So that&amp;rsquo ; s how I got introduced into this area , &amp;rdquo ; said Leuze. &amp;ldquo ; In 2008 I heard through Hermann and Janie about Enbridge and I followed the story very closely . That was the first moment when I thought someone has to tell that story &amp;mdash ; to tell the story from Hermann and Janie fighting together with the Gitga&amp;rsquo ; at First Nation against tankers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then everything changed from oil to gas , everything got really complicated , but yes that was the first moment , 2008 , when I thought this should be a film . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After Wray&amp;rsquo ; s and Meuter&amp;rsquo ; s relationship ended in 2016 , Wray went on to build the Fin Island station and the B.C. Whales organization . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We do things very differently and I think that is a good thing . It compliments both sides of things that need to be done , &amp;rdquo ; said Wray who focuses on humpbacks while Meuter has orcas in his sights . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53813;TOOLONG ... title= " Hermann Meuter , whale researcher at Cetacea Lab , at Whale Point on Gil Island , monitors the underwater noise generated by a passing tanker in an effort to collect data demonstrating the impact of increased tanker traffic on the wildlife living in the area . " alt= " Hermann Meuter , whale researcher at Cetacea Lab , at Whale Point on Gil Island , monitors the underwater noise generated by a passing tanker in an effort to collect data demonstrating the impact of increased tanker traffic on the wildlife living in the area . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of National Film Board " **28;53849;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1173943 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the current reality is LNG and increased tanker traffic is part of the area&amp;rsquo ; s future , Wray isn&amp;rsquo ; t completely without optimism . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I always believe there is still a little window of hope . I will always stand on that until I actually see a tanker , &amp;rdquo ; said Wray. &amp;ldquo ; I still have a glimmer of hope that it might not happen , probably more due to economics . I still think there is a hope for that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Right now Wray is in her usual winter mode of conferences and grant proposal but she admits every year she pines for her life of watching over the gentle giants of the sea . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t think I could choose , &amp;rdquo ; said Wray when asked about her favourite part of her job . &amp;ldquo ; I guess because I wake up every day and I am truly filled with love . I just love it . I can&amp;rsquo ; t even imagine not doing it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We all have days when we wake up and things aren&amp;rsquo ; t as bright as they should be and I just think about this opportunity and it brings me up every day . When I am going through a bad time or a hard time it just pulls me up because I feel like I have a purpose . It gives me purpose . There it is. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;53879;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;53906;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;53940;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s2 " &gt;&lt;a href= " **27;53971;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK **35;54000;TOOLONG class= " s1 " &gt; to report a typo. &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Is there more to this story ? " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55546;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171260 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BINNERS&amp;rsquo ; DINNER : In fact , a fourth annual lunch , not dinner , was served recently at Regional Recycling Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s Evans Street facility . Binners , who constitute 40 per cent of the operation&amp;rsquo ; s 200-to-300 daily clients , also chose from racks of donated warm garments . General manager Jason Smith said that non-binner depositors had waived $2,700 worth of refunds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55576;TOOLONG ... title= " Regional Recycling Vancouver staffer Hen Lai and sister Kim managed the hotdog barbecue at the Evans Street operation 's annual luncheon for binners. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55612;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171261 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The retained cash bought new underwear and socks for distribution , too . The recent doubling of some recycling fees has benefited binners , said Smith , who hopes to see deposits charged on dairy containers . Regional Recycling opens at 7:30 for binners , always provides hot coffee , and conducts a $100 cash draw every two weeks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55642;TOOLONG ... title= " Jane Durante will design a Japanese garden for architect Michael Green 's Salt Spring Island home , art studio and woodworking-shop complex . " alt= " Jane Durante will design a Japanese garden for architect Michael Green 's Salt Spring Island home , art studio and woodworking-shop complex . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55678;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171259 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; STILL BUILDING : Architect Michael Green threw another seasonal party recently . As usual , its invitation uniquely read : &amp;ldquo ; No RSVP . Guests Welcome . &amp;rdquo ; Wall-to-wall celebrants included landscape architect Janet Durante , who has a home adjacent to Green&amp;rsquo ; s two-hectare Salt Spring Island beachfront property . He has demolished a 1910 house there , retained a 1930s one , and is building a house , art studio and woodworking shop . Durante will add a Japanese-style garden . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s like the shoemaker&amp;rsquo ; s ( unshod ) children , &amp;rdquo ; wood-high-rise specialist Green said of his low-rise complex&amp;rsquo ; s leisurely development . Real-life youngsters , grads and even architectural retirees will benefit from its completion , though . Up to 25 students in Green&amp;rsquo ; s seven-year-old DBR ( Design Build Research ) non-profit program will spend two weeks there . They&amp;rsquo ; ll design and then build structures that , like their Lower Mainland predecessors , will benefit the local community . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55708;TOOLONG ... title= " The UBCP/ACTRA showbiz union named Camille Sullivan and Ben Cotton best actor and actress for roles in films Kingsway and Crown and Anchor . " alt= " The UBCP/ACTRA showbiz union named Camille Sullivan and Ben Cotton best actor and actress for roles in films Kingsway and Crown and Anchor . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55744;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171256 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; AND THE WINNERS ARE : Being honoured by your professional peers is always rewarding . It was for Ben Cotton and Camille Sullivan recently when fellow UBCP/ACTRA members named them best actor and actress at that union&amp;rsquo ; s eighth annual awards show . Cotton was cited for his role in the 2018 film Crown and Anchor , and Sullivan for hers in Bruce Sweeney&amp;rsquo ; s city-shot Kingsway. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55774;TOOLONG ... title= " Canadian Club of Vancouver president Raymond Greenwood wore a vintage Vancouver Canucks jersey to welcome former goaltender Kirk McLean . " alt= " Canadian Club of Vancouver president Raymond Greenwood wore a vintage Vancouver Canucks jersey to welcome former goaltender Kirk McLean . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55810;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171257 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; TWINE MINDED : Few Vancouver Canucks jerseys are seen at Canadian Club of Vancouver luncheons . But some were worn when club president Raymond Greenwood &amp;mdash ; a.k.a . Mr. Fireworks &amp;mdash ; had &amp;shy ; Kirk McLean reminisce on his 1987&amp;mdash ; 1998 spell as Canucks&amp;rsquo ; goaltender . There were fireworks aplenty when a riot followed McLean backstopping the 1994 Stanley Cup&amp;rsquo ; s seventh-game loss to his future team , the New York Rangers . Of the Canucks today , McLean said : &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re drafting and we&amp;rsquo ; re developing within . &amp;rdquo ; Meanwhile , the team-owning Aquilinis &amp;ldquo ; do everything they can to make players more comfortable . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55840;TOOLONG ... title= " Daniel and Henrik Sedin 's Vancouver Canucks numbers will join Pavel Bure , Trevor Linden , Markus Naslund and Stan Smyl 's when retired in February . " alt= " Daniel and Henrik Sedin 's Vancouver Canucks numbers will join Pavel Bure , Trevor Linden , Markus Naslund and Stan Smyl 's when retired in February . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55876;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171262 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for other former players , McLean noted that Daniel and Henrik Sedin&amp;rsquo ; s 22 and 33 numbers will be retired to hang with Pavel Bure , Trevor Linden , Markus Naslund and Stan Smyl&amp;rsquo ; s during weeklong February ceremonies . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55906;TOOLONG ... title= " Laura Byspalko heads a campaign for programming , funding artists and other needs of the Indian Summer Festival that she and Sirish Rao founded in 2011. " alt= " Laura Byspalko heads a campaign for programming , funding artists and other needs of the Indian Summer Festival that she and Sirish Rao founded in 2011. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;55942;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171255 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; FESTIVAL SUPPORT : The Indian Summer Festival has gained much status since Laura Byspalko and Sirish Rao founded it in 2011 . According to Rao , the diversified 14-day festival serves &amp;ldquo ; the curious mind . The more it is fed , the more curious it gets . &amp;rdquo ; Byspalko recently launched a campaign for feeding the festival itself with $25&amp;ndash ; $600 donations that would help expand programming , pay artists , keep ticket prices low and provide free access to the needy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; GIVE US AN E : Pacific Central station&amp;rsquo ; s trains and buses must be speedier than repairs to its red-neon sign&amp;rsquo ; s darkened E. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; FISH FINGER-POPPERS : A Fraser River rockfall is preventing many salmon from proceeding upstream . In the Oct. 22 , 1927 , Vancouver Sun , riverside observers blamed a similar dearth on salmon disliking jazz , possibly including Bessie Smith&amp;rsquo ; s then-current Back Water Blues . Conversely , Australia&amp;rsquo ; s Macquarie University Fish Lab recently reported that feeding sharks prefer jazz to classical airs such as Debussy&amp;rsquo ; s La Mer. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55972;TOOLONG ... title= " Lola Lush vamped with city painter Jim Cummins , aka I. Braineater , whose name and art echo currently exhibiting Thierry Guetta , aka Mr. Brainwash. " alt= " Lola Lush vamped with city painter Jim Cummins , aka I. Braineater , whose name and art echo currently exhibiting Thierry Guetta , aka Mr. Brainwash. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;56008;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171258 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; BRAIN POWER : Chali-Rosso gallerist Susanna Strem is exhibiting works by Mr. Brainwash &amp;mdash ; real name Thierry Guetta &amp;mdash ; at a McArthurGlen Designer Outlet pop-up locale . Some say the Franco-Californian artist is a creation of Britain&amp;rsquo ; s similar Banksy , whose real name may be Robin Gunningham . Vancouver has a brain-nicknamed painter in their genre , too . He&amp;rsquo ; s Jim Cummins , a.k.a . I. Braineater , who covers more canvas , wood , metal and even human skin than many house painters do walls . Perhaps Strem will show him one day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; MISSED CHANCE : One year ago , an all-Canadian Christmas present to Juan Guaid&amp;oacute ; might have changed his lot . Remember him ? Venezuela&amp;rsquo ; s presidential aspirant who , despite many nations&amp;rsquo ; encouragement , couldn&amp;rsquo ; t topple Nicol&amp;aacute ; s Maduro ? If only we&amp;rsquo ; d given him Lt.-Col D.J . Goodspeed&amp;rsquo ; s astounding book , The Conspirators . It analyzes four coups d&amp;rsquo ; etat that succeeded and four that failed , from Belgrade in 1903 to Rastenburg in 1944 . While noting that &amp;ldquo ; no ( coup ) technique yet known could succeed in a true democracy , &amp;rdquo ; The Conspirators&amp;rsquo ; most vital conclusion is : &amp;ldquo ; The nation&amp;rsquo ; s armed forces should support the rebels or at the least be neutral . &amp;rdquo ; Venezuelan troops did neither , whereupon Guaido lost his bid but , remarkably , not his life . Vancouver Public Library has one copy of Goodspeed&amp;rsquo ; s appraisal of a subject necessarily never taught in nations&amp;rsquo ; military staff colleges , so you may need a coup de chance to get it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; DOWN PARRYSCOPE : Merry Christmas to everyone , including post-NAFTA negotiators who gave us the unpronounceable USMCA instead of CAMUS OR USCAM. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **27;56038;TOOLONG " **34;56067;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 604-929-8456 &lt;/p&gt; Malcolm ParrySat , 21 Dec 2019 04:18:27 **45;56103;TOOLONG ... ArtsLocal **25;56150;TOOLONG ArtsShort film celebrates return of totem pole raising in Haida Gwaii after a century of banishmenthttp : **30;56177;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;56209;TOOLONG ... title= " Haida director Christopher Auchter 's Now Is The Time takes us through history to revisit the day in August 1969 when three generations of Eagle and Raven clan gathered to raise the first totem pole on Haida Gwaii in over a hundred years . " alt= " Haida director Christopher Auchter 's Now Is The Time takes us through history to revisit the day in August 1969 when three generations of Eagle and Raven clan gathered to raise the first totem pole on Haida Gwaii in over a hundred years . " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of NFB " **28;56245;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1168775 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Burnaby filmmaker &lt;a href= " https : **33;56275;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Christopher Auchter&lt;/a&gt; can now add the famed &lt;a href= " https : **33;56310;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Sundance Film Festival&lt;/a&gt; to the list of events that have welcomed his short film Now is the Time this past year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 16-minute film tells the story of a totem pole raising in 1969 &amp;mdash ; the first one in a century &amp;mdash ; in &lt;a href= " http : **34;56345;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Old Masset&lt;/a&gt; in &lt;a href= " https : //www.gohaidagwaii.ca/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Haida Gwaii&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Adding Sundance to a screening list that already includes &lt;a href= " https : //www.tiff.net/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;TIFF&lt;/a&gt; , the &lt;a href= " http : //viff.org " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Vancouver International Film Festival&lt;/a&gt; and a handful of other events was of course a cherry on the top of a great year for the Haida filmmaker . But what made the situation a little bit sweeter was the response Auchter got from a Sundance official when she told him Now is the Time is one of the 74 short films out of 10,000 plus submissions that made the cut for the prestigious Utah-based festival on Jan 23-Feb 2 , 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The Sundance woman I talked to really liked the movie and she had never even heard of a totem pole or Haida Gwaii , &amp;rdquo ; said Auchter. &amp;ldquo ; The fact that the story still grabbed her is great . She said : &amp;lsquo ; now I know quite a bit about totem poles . &amp;rsquo ; That was quite special to hear . That she found the story so compelling was amazing for me. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe src= " https : **34;56381;TOOLONG ... width= " 640 " height= " 360 " frameborder= " 0 " allow= " autoplay ; fullscreen " allowfullscreen title= " Now Is the Time ( Promo Clip 30sec - COMING SOON ) " &gt;&lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Along with the contemporary images , animation and interviews that Auchter shot , the film incorporates really fantastic footage from Eugene Boyko&amp;rsquo ; s 1970 NFB documentary This Was the Time that chronicled the carving and the raising of the pole . The new Auchter movie is the result of the NFB&amp;rsquo ; s inactive to get Indigenous filmmakers to create works using the Board&amp;rsquo ; s archives . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Front and centre in both the archival and modern footage is First Nations master carver and international art star Robert Davidson . It was he and his brother Reg , who at the time were 22 and 14 respectively , worked on the pole six days a week for three-and-half months . The pole , Davidson&amp;rsquo ; s first ever , still stands in Masset these five decades later . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was really special to meet them and learn about their story and interpret it through this film , &amp;rdquo ; said Auchter. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56417;TOOLONG ... title= " Twenty-two-year-old Robert Davidson and his grandfather , Tsinii , discuss the raising of the first totem pole on Haida Gwaii in over a hundred years . " alt= " Twenty-two-year-old Robert Davidson and his grandfather , Tsinii , discuss the raising of the first totem pole on Haida Gwaii in over a hundred years . " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of NFB " **28;56453;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1168773 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; That raising of the pole was considered a reawakening for the Haida people as many of the huge cedar poles&amp;nbsp ; that were all over Haida Gwaii&amp;nbsp ; a century and a half ago had been felled after missionaries&amp;nbsp ; deemed them&amp;nbsp ; sinful&amp;nbsp ; and tickets into hell . Government policies also allowed collectors and ethnologists to take poles away . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The pole raising attracted over 1,000 Haida people from the Raven and Eagle clans . Many of the elders that attended were wearing paper headpieces as their traditional wood-carved ceremonial masks , and most of their other artwork , had disappeared or was destroyed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There was really a void , but I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know the scale of the void , &amp;rdquo ; says Davidson in the film about the loss of art . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56483;TOOLONG ... title= " Haida elders gather to celebrate the raising of the first totem pole on Haida Gwaii in over a hundred years . The destruction of the community 's totem poles was part of the systemic obliteration of Haida songs , ceremonies , and culture that had occurred over the previous decades . " alt= " Haida elders gather to celebrate the raising of the first totem pole on Haida Gwaii in over a hundred years . The destruction of the community 's totem poles was part of the systemic obliteration of Haida songs , ceremonies , and culture that had occurred over the previous decades . " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of NFB " **28;56519;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1168776 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t realize how much ceremony was connected to the art until that moment , &amp;rdquo ; says Robert Davidson in the film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the Davidson&amp;rsquo ; s the carving of the pole was very much a deliberate act that managed to draw out the community&amp;rsquo ; s history . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Their part was carving the pole and once they started doing that the elders in the community stepped in and started doing their part which was doing the potlatch and figuring out how to raise the pole , &amp;rdquo ; said Auchter. &amp;ldquo ; They were all trying to piece together pieces of knowledge that each other had from their grandparents or parents &amp;mdash ; the songs the dances . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The pole was so important not just because it was a big pole but because of all the things it triggered in the songs , the dances and the potlatch ( which was made illegal in 1884 and repealed in 1951 but still remained very rare ) . The protocols , &amp;rdquo ; added Auchter. &amp;ldquo ; It was like the keys to the ignition that started the engine again in terms of the Haida culture and the arts . It was so fascinating how everything was linked together . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56549;TOOLONG ... title= " Filmmaker Christopher Auchter 's new short film Now is the Time has been accepted into the prestigious Sundance Film Festival . Photo : Tracy Auchter " alt= " Filmmaker Christopher Auchter 's new short film Now is the Time has been accepted into the prestigious Sundance Film Festival . Photo : Tracy Auchter " data-portal-copyright= " Tracy Auchter " **28;56585;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1168774 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 39-year-old Auchter &amp;mdash ; his Haida name is Waats&amp;rsquo ; daa &amp;mdash ; grew up in Haida Gwaii but he didn&amp;rsquo ; t grow up with this story . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s this shock that I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know about this story , &amp;rdquo ; said Auchter whose Raven clan is called Yahgulanaas. &amp;ldquo ; The bear mother pole , is always there but how come I didn&amp;rsquo ; t know about this ? It seemed like such a big moment for us . I just thought it would be a story that would be talked about more . Whether it is in our school or around our kitchen table . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What was so fascinating was unearthing the story , all of the story , and being able to share , &amp;rdquo ; added Auchter. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Festivals aside the film , that is also part of the &lt;a href= " https : **30;56615;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;NFB 's educational kit&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now the film will get to touch a wider audience with its upcoming Sundance run on Jan. 26-28 and Jan. 31. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For me personally it was such a gift to spend this time to learn this part of my history and then be able to share it , &amp;rdquo ; said Auchter. &amp;ldquo ; I also tried to do it in a way that brings not just the Haida community in , but do it in a way that it will bring in other people , other groups within Canada and now in the U.S. and hopefully beyond . So it&amp;rsquo ; s not just an inside story . So it is a story of hope , resilience . A happy story . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Auchter , who is a talented animator ( The Mountain of SGaana is an award-winning film he wrote , directed and produced ) , is currently working on a graphic novel/Haida manga book called Noble One . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s an old Haida story that explains why there are earthquakes on Haida Gwaii , &amp;rdquo ; said Auchter , who is also planning more work in film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I am looking forward to directing a new film again shortly but exactly what is , is still being formed . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;56647;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;56674;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;56708;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeFri , 20 Dec 2019 19:06:46 **45;56739;TOOLONG ... ArtsMoviesPixomondo Vancouver 's VFX breakdown reel for Apple TV 's See really rockshttp : **35;57204;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;57241;TOOLONG ... title= " Jason Momoa stars with Alfre Woodard in Apple TV 's See , which features effects by Vancouver company Pixomondo. " alt= " Jason Momoa stars with Alfre Woodard in Apple TV 's See , which features effects by Vancouver company Pixomondo. " data-portal-copyright= " MARK RALSTON " **28;57277;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1171249 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Vancouver-shot Apple TV original series See stars Alfre Woodard and Jason Momoa . It premiered on the streaming platform on Nov. 1 . The story follows a society in a distant future where the human race has lost the sense of sight and what happens when two twins are born who can see . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Vancouver office of Pixomondo recently released a visual-effects reel to showcase a scene in the first episode of See . Pixomondo VFX supervisor Matthew Welford commented on the work that was done for the key scene in the VFX reel . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In Episode One , we encounter a giant battle of two of the warring tribes when an army of Witchfinders attacks the village of Alkenny . After a bloody and gruesome fight , Baba Voss ( Jason Momoa ) is forced to unleash the final line of defence before his tribe is overrun , &amp;rdquo ; said Welford . &amp;ldquo ; This means causing a rock-wall avalanche down the side of the mountainside. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;57307;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;57343;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Pixomondo&amp;rsquo ; s Vancouver division scanned the actual hillside location before practical photography commenced . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Then we recreated a fully CG rock wall made up of thousands of individual rocks , mud , plates and debris , &amp;rdquo ; said Welford . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We used FX simulation to accurately recreate how those rocks ( which he had scanned earlier ) would actually roll down the hillside . It was necessary to make sure the rocks interacted with both the world around them , and actors within the scene , for the final shots . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The final shots comprised of CGI rocks , digital humans , digital plants , digital debris all integrated with the live-action actors and plates shot on location . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a pretty fascinating process to watch . Once again , it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to tell where the real and the created come together as Vancouver&amp;rsquo ; s VFX expertise continues to amaze. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;57379;TOOLONG " **36;57410;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;57448;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;57477;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynTue , 17 Dec 2019 01:55:54 **45;57508;TOOLONG ... There he delivered a Christmas turkey-and-trimmings meal to Streetfront Alternative Middle School teacher Trevor Stokes and the sometimes-hard-done-by students he says &amp;ldquo ; are worth investing in. &amp;rdquo ; The event was staged by Vancouver Firefighters Charities members who partner the non-mainstream school through the Sports for Kids program .
