" data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;57761;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1164689 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; TIED TO BE FIT : Like apples , accomplished parents&amp;rsquo ; children reputedly land close to the tree . The moviebiz equivalent is not being left on the cutting-room floor . That&amp;rsquo ; s the case with Amanda Giannakos , whose mother , Gabriela Schonbach , is partner-executive producer at city-based Omnifilm Entertainment . At that firm&amp;rsquo ; s recent 40th anniversary celebration , marketing-distribution director Giannakos said she founded the independent but related NM Media Co. in April . Its first 25-episode series , Strong By NM , will air next fall on the One Get Fit channel . It presently carries Omnifilm&amp;rsquo ; s Namaste Yoga series that former lawyer Giannakos heads . Early-morning one-minute handstands may have concentrated ( or frazzled ) her brain to launch the 160-paper-page Movement by NM magazine that &amp;ldquo ; explores the intersection of art , fitness and everyday life . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57791;TOOLONG ... title= " Marc Schutzbank , who heads the Fresh Roots student-farming programs , received $13,000 from charity-funding Give A Damn founder Martin McNish. " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;57827;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1164686 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; DAMN RIGHT : Odlum Brown portfolio manager Martin McNish and others gave charity giving a new twist in 2016 by sparkplugging the Give A Damn program . At quarterly events , twenties-to-forties-aged members each put up $100 and vote on pitches by three charities&amp;rsquo ; representatives . In the Yaletown&amp;rsquo ; s Earls loft recently , Fresh Roots ( freshroots.ca ) executive director Marc Schutzbank , 32 , received a $13,000 pot swelled by attendees and McNish&amp;rsquo ; s friend Martin Jones , the San Jose Sharks goaltender . Schutzbank , whose wife Ilana Labow co-founded Fresh Roots in 2009 , said elementary and secondary students grow food at educational farms on several schools&amp;rsquo ; grounds , learn to cook it and see it go to cafeterias , food-access programs and suchlike . &amp;ldquo ; When kids are outside and growing something , they also find success in the classroom , and their confidence increases , &amp;rdquo ; said U.S.-native Schutzbank who came to UBC in 2010 on a Fulbright scholarship . &amp;ldquo ; If they grow it , they will eat it , &amp;rdquo ; he said of Fresh Roots&amp;rsquo ; students . Beginning in fall 2020 , work will involve wraparound terraces on both floors . Daniel , who runs the family firm today , also owns all three Tap &amp;amp ; Barrel pub-restaurants and Brewhall , the former Steel Toad Brewery . " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;58025;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1164685 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; DAY&amp;rsquo ; S AHEAD : Victoria-born former logger David Day has written and helped provide paper for more books than many have read : 50 and counting . His Tolkien Bestiary sold a million copies . His Doomsday Book of Animals , with a foreword by the Duke of Edinburgh , sold 750,000 . Pal Terry Jones , the Monty Python Flying Circus team member , wrote the introduction for his Decoding Wonderland . Three more in Day&amp;rsquo ; s Tolkien series were released recently in North America , the UK and France . With wife Roisin , the long-time expatriate will soon occupy a former Day-family home in Victoria . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58055;TOOLONG ... title= " Journalist-author Stevie Cameron 's brother , artist-drummer-singer Chris Dahl , has released the Silver Dagger single from his Smoke + Shadows album " alt= " Journalist-author Stevie Cameron 's brother , artist-drummer-singer Chris Dahl , has released the Silver Dagger single from his Smoke + Shadows album " data-portal-copyright= " Malcolm Parry " **28;58091;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1164684 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; HI-YO SILVER : Further up-Island , Qualicum Beach&amp;rsquo ; s Ceramics Artworks firm owner Chris Dahl has released the Silver Dagger title track of a self-produced world-fusion album titled Smoke + Shadows . " " data-portal-copyright= " Photo Credit : Universal Studios " **28;58594;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1163151 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dinosaurs of Jurassic World can go anywhere now . With only minor exceptions , the creatures had been enisled for most of the franchise&amp;rsquo ; s five films , but at the conclusion of 2018&amp;rsquo ; s Fallen Kingdom , several dinosaurs escaped . Now there&amp;rsquo ; s no telling where they&amp;rsquo ; ll turn up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some locations have already been confirmed . The Jurassic Park universe descends on Vancouver this spring , for instance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The third instalment in the Jurassic World franchise , tentatively titled &lt;em&gt;Arcadia , &lt;/em&gt; will film all over the globe next year , with shoots planned at London&amp;rsquo ; s Pinewood Studios and the islands of Oahu and Malta . The production will also spend two weeks in Hollywood North , filming around Metro Vancouver between Feb. 24 and Mar. 6. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If you thought it was weird seeing &lt;a href= " http : **34;58624;TOOLONG ... bodies and zombie horses in downtown Vancouver&lt;/a&gt; last month , just wait until Gastown is beset by velociraptors. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58660;TOOLONG ... title= " Claire and Franklin encounter the Baryonyx in " Jurassic World : Fallen Kingdom . " " alt= " Claire and Franklin encounter the Baryonyx in " Jurassic World : Fallen Kingdom . " " data-portal-copyright= " Photo Credit : Universal Pictures " **28;58696;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1163147 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Keep your eyes peeled for the film&amp;rsquo ; s A-list cast of humans as well . &amp;nbsp ; In addition to returning stars Chris Pratt and Bryce Dallas Howard , who play the trilogy&amp;rsquo ; s leads , Owen Grady and Claire Dearing , the third film will also include appearances from original Jurassic Park cast members Laura Dern , Sam Neill , Jeff Goldblum , and BD Wong. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also returning is writer/director Colin Trevorrow , who penned and helmed the first Jurassic World film before ceding the chair to J.A . Bayona for the 2018 sequel , based on a Trevorrow script . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trevorrow has also co-written the third instalment of the franchise , so it&amp;rsquo ; s only fitting he should be the one to bring this trilogy home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Don&amp;rsquo ; t be surprised if Arcadia gets a new name before its release . For now , the title makes some sense , as it was also the name of the boat that transported the dinosaurs off Isla Nublar in Fallen Kingdom . But previous films in the franchise have also used working titles to conceal their productions : Jurassic World was briefly titled Ebb Tide , and Fallen Kingdom was originally called Ancient Futures . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The original Jurassic World , released in 2015 , earned $1.6 billion worldwide on a budget of $150 million , while its 2018 sequel , Fallen Kingdom , earned $1.3 billion on a $170 million budget . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The budget for Jurassic World 3 has not been released . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The movie is slated to hit theatres on June 11 , 2021. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **28;58726;TOOLONG " **35;58756;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **28;58793;TOOLONG " class= " twitter-follow-button " data-show-count= " false " &gt;Follow **25;58823;TOOLONG &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;&lt;a href= " **27;58850;TOOLONG " &gt;CLICK HERE&lt;/a&gt; to report a typo. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Is there more to this story ? ArtsMoviesPressure to marry and have kids is still weirdly a thing as new Chelsea Peretti film points outhttp : **37;58993;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;59032;TOOLONG ... title= " Chelsea Peretti ( l ) and Nadia Tonen play aunt and niece in the new comedy Spinster . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Spinster production " **28;59068;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1158120 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Whistler Film Festival&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; on until Dec. 8 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Whistler &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;59098;TOOLONG ? gclid ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;59127;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When director &lt;a href= " https : //andreadorfman.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt; Andrea Dorfman&lt;/a&gt; and screenwriter &lt;a href= " https : **30;59161;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Jennifer Deyell&lt;/a&gt; put the word out for a late thirty-something female actor to star in their new film Spinster it was clear they as filmmakers they were in a good position but sadly the film business itself still had a long way to go . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We connected with a casting agent in L.A. and sure enough there were a lot of people available , &amp;rdquo ; said Dorfman over the phone from Halifax recently . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That deep pool of available female talent comes as no surprise as it is well documented that over 30 means over the Hollywood hill . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59193;TOOLONG ... title= " Filmmaker Andrea Dorfman 's fourth feature Spinster looks at one woman 's struggle to break free of the idea that she needs to get married and have kids . " alt= " Filmmaker Andrea Dorfman 's fourth feature Spinster looks at one woman 's struggle to break free of the idea that she needs to get married and have kids . " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Spinster production " **28;59229;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1158117 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t surprised at all , &amp;rdquo ; said the Halifax-based director before referencing a hilarious skit from the TV show Inside &lt;a href= " https : //www.amyschumer.com/ " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Amy Schumer&lt;/a&gt; . In the skit titled Last F&amp;amp ; *%able Day Tina Fey , Julia Louis-Dreyfus and Patricia Arquette are seated at a fully-stocked dining table in a clearing in the woods . While out walking Schumer comes across them and joins them at the table . The raise their glasses in a toast and Schumer asks if it is someone&amp;rsquo ; s birthday ? Nope , it&amp;rsquo ; s not . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s kind of the opposite . We&amp;rsquo ; re celebrating Julia&amp;rsquo ; s last f$*k%ble day , &amp;rdquo ; says Arquette . Schumer asks what that means . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In every actresses life the media decides when you finally reach the point when you are not believably f$*k%ble anymore , &amp;rdquo ; says Dreyfus. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The skit was a big hit and a big knowing wink to the stark reality of female ageism in the entertainment business . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s in the air . We know that women fall off of a cliff after a certain age . You have 50-year-old male actors getting together on camera with 25-year-olds . It&amp;rsquo ; s crazy . It&amp;rsquo ; s so in your face , &amp;rdquo ; said Dorfman before adding a sliver of hope to the discussion . &amp;ldquo ; I also think things are changing . More and more women are becoming directors and sticking as directors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think the more women telling stories and more different kinds of people telling stories ( will lead to ) definitely seeing more employment , I hope , for older women , &amp;rdquo ; said Dorfman who is also an animator , illustrator , documentary filmmaker and screenwriter. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While there is still a way to go to reach parity the discussion around the increase in female directors is very much a thing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here in North America names like Lulu Wang , Olivia Wilde , Annie Silverstein , Semi Chellas and Melina Matsoukas are just a handful of many up-in-coming female directors that have garnered attention recently and are poised to join the ranks of today&amp;rsquo ; s well established working Hollywood directors like Ava DeVernay , Greta Gerwig , Elizabeth Banks , Kathryn Bigelow , Patty Jenkins , Sarah Polley and others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , the likes of Jenkins and Anna Boden aside , it seems the big tent pole pictures are still for the most part the purview of male directors . Where women directors are the norm is the indie film world . Those films get their first lives on festival screens like the ones operated by the Whistler Film Festival ( WFF ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now in its 19th year , the festival wraps on Dec. 8 after five days of top-notch cinema , events and programs , the WFF is pretty good in the non-male director department . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This year 40 per cent of the festival&amp;rsquo ; s 86 films ( features and shorts ) are directed or co-directed by women , or non-binary individuals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dorfman is one of 13 women at the helm of a feature at this year&amp;rsquo ; s festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s amazing . That&amp;rsquo ; s fantastic . I love hearing that , &amp;rdquo ; said Dorfman. &amp;ldquo ; I see a lot of women make first and second features but I don&amp;rsquo ; t always see the stick to-it-ness that maybe our male colleagues have . That&amp;rsquo ; s what I would like to see . I would love to see what women make over their career . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spinster was co-conceived with Dorfman&amp;rsquo ; s longtime creative partner Deyell . About 15 years ago Dorfman and Deyell noticed that there was a discussion swirling around them and other women of a certain age . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In my mid 30s it was a time when all my friends were getting married and having kids and I was single , &amp;rdquo ; said Dorfman. &amp;ldquo ; She ( Jennifer ) also had a good friend who was single at that time and for both of us it was a really hard place to be . There was this mad race ( that lead ) to a lot of friends to settle just to make it happen . I was like I am &amp;lsquo ; not settling , &amp;rsquo ; and her friend was very much the same way . We would commiserate about this and talk about it endlessly and that I think was what the initial script was born out of . Just this idea that you are at the top of your game in many ways and still women can feel bad about being single which is absurd . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This film is product of lots of chat between Deyell and Dorfman not just as filmmakers looking for a good story , but as women looking and hoping to change the story . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I always say I want to see films I wish had been around when I was at a certain moment in my life , &amp;rdquo ; added Dorfman who has a boyfriend and two stepkids. &amp;ldquo ; Maybe this will be that film . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A character in the film was inspired by the friend of Jennifer&amp;rsquo ; s who Dorfman said was a scientist who studied bats in the Amazon and had &amp;ldquo ; this incredible life . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Even though she had this amazing life and was so successful her friends were still like : &amp;lsquo ; ahhhh she&amp;rsquo ; s not married . ahhhh she doesn&amp;rsquo ; t have kids . &amp;rsquo ; The fact that we do that to women over and over again is terrible . That we can give them praise for their lives then take it away from them because they don&amp;rsquo ; t have the marriage , the kids , the house ? Once again it is feeding into a fallacy because women have incredibly rewarding and successful lives while not being mothers and married . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Made for $1 million Spinster &amp;mdash ; which had its world premiere at the Whistler festival on Dec. 6 and will play again on Dec. 8 &amp;mdash ; is all about that pressure . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Out of the deep talent pool of thirty-something actors that were available for work Dorfman and Deyell cast American comedian/actor Chelsea Peretti as Gaby the 39-year-old catering company owner who dreams of her own restaurant while navigating the sad sack looks and pity pouts she gets from those contemplating her singleness. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59259;TOOLONG ... title= " Actors Chelsea Peretti and Jonathan Watton in a scene from the new film Spinster . The Halifax shot comedy is part of the 2019 Whistler Film Festival that wraps its 19th season on Dec. 8 , 2019. " alt= " Actors Chelsea Peretti and Jonathan Watton in a scene from the new film Spinster . The Halifax shot comedy is part of the 2019 Whistler Film Festival that wraps its 19th season on Dec. 8 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Courtesy of Spinster production " **28;59295;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1158118 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I enjoy women in power so I wanted to be directed by Andrea , &amp;rdquo ; said Peretti via email . &amp;ldquo ; When I got the offer , I watched her films and thought they looked beautiful . She has a specific vibe and style and she made me fall in love with Nova Scotia . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What Dorfman liked about Peretti was her willingness and eagerness to get right into the mix of the film . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; She was a participant and collaborator , &amp;rdquo ; said Dorfman about Peretti ( Brooklyn Nine-Nine and Parks and Recreation ) adding that well received standup comedian asked if she could make some of the jokes her own . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I know I&amp;rsquo ; m mouthy . I&amp;rsquo ; m irritable and I need to work on my core , &amp;rdquo ; says Gaby to guy who is leaving her after she has desperately and wrongly amped up a three-month relationship . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shot over three week in Halifax in the summer of 2018 Spinster is a funny and sometimes melancholy look at one woman&amp;rsquo ; s evolution through and out of the archaic social structures that tell women they need a partner and kids to be truly happy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thankfully Gaby finally gets it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s weird marriage started as a contract of ownership and now it has kind of evolved into at best gross consumerism at worst a tax cut , &amp;rdquo ; says Gaby . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Peretti married filmmaker/comedian/actor Jordan Peele in 2016 when she was 38 . They had a son in 2017 but Peretti said her biological clock wasn&amp;rsquo ; t ringing and she wasn&amp;rsquo ; t facing down the pressure to get the partner and kid . She was lucky . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t sure what the future was going to hold for me &amp;mdash ; I really enjoyed my work and the community of comedians and actors I got to be around who are some of the funniest , most entertaining people in the world , &amp;rdquo ; said Peretti. &amp;ldquo ; I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t sure which way my life was going to go but felt like it was going to be exciting in whichever direction it went . I was right . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spoiler alert : this film doesn&amp;rsquo ; t get tied up with a big rom com bow and that makes for some fantastic post flick chat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think it is timely , &amp;rdquo ; said Dorfman. &amp;ldquo ; Jennifer and I have always felt we want to make films that people can go out for a coffee afterwards and talk about it . I think this is one of those films that people are going to have something to say about it , positive or negative . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Peretti added that she was thrilled to play the type of woman she wants to see on the big screen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I am honoured I was given the opportunity to embody this character , &amp;rdquo ; said Peretti. &amp;ldquo ; I hope women and anyone watching will leave emboldened to explore more new experiences . Romantic comedies never really resonated with me so I hope Spinster offers people an alternative model or vision . There is no one way to do life . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Put that last thought on a T-shirt . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;59325;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;59352;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/danagee " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;59386;TOOLONG &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Dana GeeMon , 02 Dec 2019 19:00:53 **45;59417;TOOLONG ... ArtsMoviesWhistler Film Festival : Fall Back Down a punk rom-com first feature for SB Edwardshttp : **33;59464;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;59499;TOOLONG ... title= " Andrew Dunbar and Aadila Dosani star in the new SB Edwards film Fall Back Down . The film is part of this year 's Whistler Film Festival on at the resort until Dec. 8. " alt= " Andrew Dunbar and Aadila Dosani star in the new SB Edwards film Fall Back Down . The film is part of this year 's Whistler Film Festival on at the resort until Dec. 8. " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of WFF " **28;59535;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1152857 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Fall Back Down&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Dec. 5 at 8.30 p.m. , Dec. 6&amp;nbsp ; at 3 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Village 8 Cinemas , Whistler &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;59565;TOOLONG ? gclid ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;59594;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Director &lt;a href= " https : **33;59628;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;SB Edwards&lt;/a&gt; is a little anxious about premiering her new film &lt;a href= " https : **33;59663;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Fall Back Down&lt;/a&gt; at the 2019 &lt;a href= " https : **27;59698;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Whistler Film Festival . &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What makes the Dec. 5 world debut of her new work even more memorable is that it is the first feature-length film from the Vancouver/Los Angeles based filmmaker. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I mean the barf emoji comes to mind , &amp;rdquo ; said Edwards who also wrote the screenplay and was the lead producer on the project . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m trying not to be nervous . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Edwards is not alone in the newbie category , as 10 other directors will premiere their first features at Whistler this year . Edwards is also one of 13 female feature directors bringing work to the festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now in its 19th year , the five-day festival is showing 86 films from 15 different countries . It also boasts the largest Canadian content &amp;mdash ; almost 70 per cent &amp;mdash ; of any film festival in Canada . The festival also offers creative and professional development panels and events . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59727;TOOLONG ... title= " SB Edwards . " alt= " SB Edwards . " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of WFF " **28;59763;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1152856 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Fall Back Down marks a turning point for Edwards , she is no stranger to the filmmaking process as she has 10 years of movie/TV art department experience , short filmmaking and writing credits for TV on her resume . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But taking the reins of a full feature production , including launching it into the world , is another animal completely &amp;mdash ; something that is not lost on Edwards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; &amp;hellip ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t really know what I am doing so this whole process has felt like I am just in a constant state of about to be blindsided by something , which is a bit nerve-racking , &amp;rdquo ; said Edwards . &amp;ldquo ; But I think it is going to be wonderful because I feel really proud of how the film turned out . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film&amp;rsquo ; s leading actor Andrew Dunbar , who is also a producer on the project , shares Edwards&amp;rsquo ; feelings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Nobody has seen it yet &amp;mdash ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve seen it a 100 times &amp;mdash ; so it&amp;rsquo ; s such an interesting place to be , &amp;rdquo ; said Dunbar , whose acting resume is a testament to a busy career in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m so excited that it is going to be screened at the festival ; we&amp;rsquo ; ll get some sort of closure . We&amp;rsquo ; ll get to see it screened , and then it&amp;rsquo ; s the start of the next chapter for the film . It&amp;rsquo ; s nerve-racking . It&amp;rsquo ; s super exciting . It&amp;rsquo ; s all the feels , &amp;rdquo ; said Dunbar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59793;TOOLONG ... title= " Actor Andrew Dunbar and director/screenwriter SB Edwards on set of Fall Back Down . " alt= " Actor Andrew Dunbar and director/screenwriter SB Edwards on set of Fall Back Down . " data-portal-copyright= " courtesy of WFF " **28;59829;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1152858 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Set in Vancouver , Fall Back Down is a romantic comedy with a punk rock pastiche . The story follows Nick , a heartbroken and listless activist who has decided to not invest himself in anything anymore . That leaden outlook has resulted in an employment record littered with firings from crummy jobs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally he manages to secure a gig at a garment sweatshop , and meets the bristly , quick-tempered Reena ( Aadila Dosani ) . &amp;nbsp ; At first the pair clash but all that changes when they discover a serious crime , a crime Reena is convinced that if they expose it they will become victims of the &amp;ldquo ; textile mafia . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nick&amp;rsquo ; s activism and the activists he is close to are familiar territory for Edwards . She called a &amp;ldquo ; punk rock house , &amp;rdquo ; home and has been an activist since she was young . She has been on the protest lines for a wide range of causes more times than she can count , and is still active in making her voice heard . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; For me , it started out being about the world I was in and the communities that I was in , &amp;rdquo ; said Edwards , who began thinking about the story 10 years ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I felt the need to put us on screen and celebrate activists who protest . It&amp;rsquo ; s kind of interesting that the timing for that is more topical now . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Without becoming too preachy &amp;mdash ; because I have seen many a film , an activist based film , go down with the preachiness &amp;mdash ; I wanted to paint everyone that I feel like are my people in this noble light , &amp;rdquo ; said Edwards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another point that Edwards wanted to make in the film is how people react to big emotional upheavals and how the rest of society has a tendency toward wanting people to just get over things . That&amp;rsquo ; s not happening in this story as Nick really struggles after his radical and charming girlfriend ( Jacky Lai ) heads off on a humanitarian mission and never contacts him again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What I wanted to talk about , too , is how slow I think emotional recovery is , &amp;rdquo ; said Edwards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; In media something happens and people get better . It all gets wrapped up in an hour . I just wanted to look a little more at how long these periods are when we are mostly falling and then just slightly getting a little more healthier and a little more capable and then one day you kind of look up and you are over something you have been struggling with . When you finally reach a point when you can move in a new direction . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;59859;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;59901;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;59937;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;59968;TOOLONG ... North : The latest from the B.C. TV and film **29;60004;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;60035;TOOLONG ... the latest Metro Vancouver arts **25;60071;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Made for a very tight $200,000 , with $120,000 of that coming from a Telefilm Canada grant , the film focuses on Nick&amp;rsquo ; s bumpy journey . Played perfectly by Dunbar , Nick is one of those sad-sack characters that avoids coming off as a whiner thanks to some emotional depth and humour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For Dunbar , getting a character like Nick was a gift that every actor loves to unwrap. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t get to go out for roles like this . Casting directors don&amp;rsquo ; t see me as the punk activist , usually , &amp;rdquo ; said Dunbar who has also been named one the UBCP/Actra Stars to Watch at this year&amp;rsquo ; s festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Edwards knew Dunbar before this project , and she knew he could take some punk rock roughing up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I always knew he would do a good job , but I was shocked and thrilled and had that feeling , you know when something feels kind of magical . He is a star , &amp;rdquo ; said Edwards who also gave big props to Dosani and Lai . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You don&amp;rsquo ; t worry . You don&amp;rsquo ; t worry his performance is going to fall apart at any point . You just get to settle in and be carried by this lovely human who goes through these various emotional challenges . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the film&amp;rsquo ; s spotlight is on Nick , there is plenty to watch in the surrounding characters who keep Nick from falling further into his pit of despair . It&amp;rsquo ; s these characters that Edwards hopes to give more to do in the future . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My dream is that Fall Back Down magically turns into a spinoff TV show so I can write all the characters I didn&amp;rsquo ; t have time to explore in the movie , &amp;rdquo ; said Edwards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dunbar said that Edwards personal experience of life in a punk rock house is packed with plot ideas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Don&amp;rsquo ; t you just want to see that world ? You can dig so deep into it , &amp;rdquo ; said Dunbar , &amp;nbsp ; who just finished the Netflix film There&amp;rsquo ; s Someone Inside Your House . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The TV show idea aside , Edwards is busy working again with Fall Back Down executive producer Chris Moore ( Manchester by the Sea and Good Will Hunting ) on developing another TV show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Called Nowhere , the show is about a fraternal male and female twins who were bullied growing up in small town . There is murder and mystery , says Edwards , who is the creator and head writer on the project . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;60098;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;60140;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? ArtsMoviesSimon Pegg delivers strong dramatic turn in 2019 Whistler Film Festival openerhttp : **34;60583;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;60619;TOOLONG ... title= " Juno Temple and Simon Pegg play Hannah and Theo in the film Lost Transmissions by writer/director Katharine O'Brien . " alt= " Juno Temple and Simon Pegg play Hannah and Theo in the film Lost Transmissions by writer/director Katharine O'Brien . " data-portal-copyright= " Lost Transmissions " **28;60655;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1151907 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Whistler Film Festival&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Dec. 4-8 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Whistler &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;60685;TOOLONG ? gclid ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **32;60714;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **30;60748;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Simon Pegg&lt;/a&gt; was meant to star in the new indie film &lt;a href= " https : **33;60780;TOOLONG ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Lost Transmissions&lt;/a&gt; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was a perfect storm of reasons , &amp;rdquo ; said Pegg over the phone from London recently . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I get scripts from my agents that say &amp;lsquo ; you may or may not like this . &amp;rsquo ; But this one said &amp;lsquo ; read this&amp;rsquo ; . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then , something else caught the English actor/writer&amp;rsquo ; s eye . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I noticed it was directed by Katharine ( &lt;a href= " https : **30;60815;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Katharine O'Brien&lt;/a&gt; who also wrote the screenplay ) . I hadn&amp;rsquo ; t , until that point , been directed in a feature film by a woman , which felt remiss to me and indicative of the way the film industry is . So I was very happy when I read it and really responded to it , &amp;rdquo ; said Pegg . &amp;ldquo ; I thought it was a really moving and interesting story . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The moviemaking experience worked out well , and Pegg felt right at home working on O&amp;rsquo ; Brien&amp;rsquo ; s production . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I live in a matriarchy . Everyone in my house is female , even the animals , &amp;rdquo ; said Pegg , whose long list of film credits includes comedy &lt;a href= " https : **31;60847;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;Shaun of the Dead&lt;/a&gt; and a handful of titles in the Mission Impossible and Star Wars franchises. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Lost Transmissions story revolves around Theo ( Pegg ) and Hannah ( Juno Temple ) as Hannah tries to help the schizophrenic Theo during a psychotic break . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Off his meds , the manic Theo races through an uneasy and sepia-toned Los Angeles with Hannah and other friends hot on his heels trying to get him to help . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;60880;TOOLONG ... title= " Simon Pegg and Juno Temple in Lost Transmissions " alt= " Simon Pegg and Juno Temple in Lost Transmissions " data-portal-copyright= " Lost Transmissions " **28;60916;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1151906 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Theo&amp;rsquo ; s story is based on a friend of O&amp;rsquo ; Brien&amp;rsquo ; s who she and other close pals helped to get back to his home in England where he could get health care and treatment . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was a success story . I finally learned something this go around and I thought it was worth turning into a film , &amp;rdquo ; said O&amp;rsquo ; Brien , who has had others close to her with mental health issues . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The film will be opening this year&amp;rsquo ; s Whistler Film Festival on Dec. 4 in its Canadian debut . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The festival , now in its 19th year , has 43 features and 43 shorts from 15 countries on 2019 program . The festival will also host various special events and industry conferences and meetups. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; O&amp;rsquo ; Brien and Pegg will both be at the festival and will be on hand for a Q&amp;amp ; A after the screening . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; One of the most rewarding things about festivals is to connect to the audience after a screening , &amp;rdquo ; said O&amp;rsquo ; Brien over the phone from Los Angeles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; So much gets built up around the red carpets and all the things that go around with it , and you know at the end of the day all that stuff is so fleeting and not really real , and it is way more rewarding to talk to people that come up and say they have a brother , a wife with schizophrenia and talk about how much the film meant to them . That&amp;rsquo ; s been great . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;60946;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;60982;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; O&amp;rsquo ; Brien is one of 13 female feature film directors at the festival . She is also in the class of 11 first-time feature directors showing work at Whistler . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think I had built up enough experience that it just felt like it was time , &amp;rdquo ; said O&amp;rsquo ; Brien , who has made many award-winning short films . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It is one of those things that , I think , as a woman you have to do so much more in order to prove to people that you are ready , so I felt when my time had come I definitely gained the experience and I felt prepared to do it . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pegg , who has what the internet calls the holy trinity of sci-fi under his belt with his roles in the Dr. Who TV series and the Star Trek and Star Wars films , said O&amp;rsquo ; Brien had the 19-day shoot totally under control and looking back at the experience he is grateful the role gave him a chance to flex a creative muscles he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t often get to use . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was flattered and surprised that Katharine sent me the script because people don&amp;rsquo ; t tend to associate me with dramatic roles , &amp;rdquo ; said Pegg . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was touched because I really love doing dramatic roles . I wanted to be an actor I didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to be a comedian. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; O&amp;rsquo ; Brien , though , did not want Pegg to play Theo completely straight . The character is a charming bon vivant music producer until his illness takes over . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; He was written to be a certain way &amp;mdash ; extremely likable . He does so much pushing people away from him in the film that I knew I really knew I needed someone you were really rooting for the whole time , &amp;rdquo ; said O&amp;rsquo ; Brien. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s why Simon came to mind . It made sense to look for a comedic actor that could walk the line between something very serious that had these comedic accents , and Simon is so adept at doing that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;61018;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;61060;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? Check out our comprehensive event **29;61096;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;61127;TOOLONG ... North : The latest from the B.C. TV and film **29;61163;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;61194;TOOLONG ... the latest Metro Vancouver arts **25;61230;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; While there is humour lurking in the corners of this movie , the heft of the narrative is the dire situation of this illness and the lack of help within the stalled American medical system . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is not universal health care , and if you&amp;rsquo ; re mentally ill you are not going to be able to pay for your own health care , &amp;rdquo ; said O&amp;rsquo ; Brien reflecting on the experience of helping her friend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is just no safety net . Also the laws that are in place are outdated . The periods of observation are minimal , and it&amp;rsquo ; s just allowing a lot of people to just fall out onto the streets . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; O&amp;rsquo ; Brien said the people at the L.A. Mission in downtown Los Angeles told her they estimate 70-80 per cent of the people they see are mentally ill. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pegg , as well , sees the direct relationship between mental illness and homelessness. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have been in L.A. over the years , and Vancouver as well , and noted it there . The homeless problem and the mental health problem together form a really critical part of each city&amp;rsquo ; s social well-being , &amp;rdquo ; said Pegg , who was well versed in the issue of the historical closing of mental health facilities in Vancouver as a component to the homeless crisis . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Over a handful of hectic days you see in this film how precarious life is for someone who is mentally ill . Theo at first has it all going on , but soon the cracks appear as the medications wear off . In turn regular daily activities go out the window as psychosis creeps in. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Three bad days , &amp;rdquo ; said Pegg with a sigh . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s very tempting when you are walking down the street and you see someone on the corner just yelling at the sky as being insane . ( But ) insanity as it is , which is a sort of broad slightly reductive term , is the easiest way to marginalize anybody . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We do it all the time . You hear it all the time in politics and in art . People are branded as crazy then they&amp;rsquo ; re disqualified as human beings . But that person who is shouting might three days ago have been a perfectly functioning , well put together functioning member of society who just so happened could not afford their medication , or have been evicted from their apartment then just suddenly they&amp;rsquo ; re there and yet we just drive past them and think &amp;lsquo ; ahhh they&amp;rsquo ; re just mad . &amp;rsquo ; &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pegg , who has suffered from depression , did his research to prepare for the role of Theo . He talked with patients , their caregivers and even the man the story is based on , who Pegg reports is on his medication and doing well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His work has paid off as his Theo is a complex character that one minute is charming and lovely then the next manipulative and scary . Your heart breaks when you see him descending , and it is stomped on even further when you see what awaits the mentally ill in America . This is not an easy film , and that&amp;rsquo ; s good because no one needs that old brilliant but mad artist trope. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For this film and his impressive and varied career Pegg will be honoured at this year&amp;rsquo ; s WFF with a Maverick Award . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They know how to tempt actors , &amp;rdquo ; said Pegg with a laugh . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They didn&amp;rsquo ; t need to do that ; I would have happily packed my snowboard and gone . " data-portal-copyright= " UBCP/ACTRA handout " **28;61924;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1153880 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hollywood North&amp;rsquo ; s best and brightest were honoured Saturday at the &lt;span class= " peekhighlight " &gt;UBCP&lt;/span&gt; / &lt;span class= " peekhighlight " &gt;ACTRA&lt;/span&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;span class= " peekhighlight " &gt;awards&lt;/span&gt; in Vancouver . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Camille Sullivan was named best actress for her starring role in the comedy-drama Kingsway , while Ben Cotton earned the best actor nod for his role in the crime thriller Crown and Anchor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Steven Roberts , one of the stars of the drama In God I Trust , was named best emerging performer while Erin Mathews was honoured with the best voice acting award for Super Monsters Furever Friends . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Best stunt award went to stunt coordinator Eli Zagoudakis and stunt performers Nathaniel Shuker and Marshall Bingham for their performances in Arrow &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Lorena Gale Woman of Distinction Award was presented to actress Lesley Ewen and the John Juliani Award of Excellence was presented to actor Mel Tuck . &amp;nbsp ; Both honours are for lifetime achievement . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The UBCP/ACTRA awards gala took place at the Vancouver Playhouse and was hosted by Scrubs and The Flash actor Tom Cavanagh. &lt;/p&gt; Staff ReporterTue , 26 Nov 2019 04:25:45 **45;61954;TOOLONG ... **29;62001;TOOLONG Irish Film Festival adds some Oscar sparkle for second yearhttp : **36;62032;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;62070;TOOLONG ... title= " ? ine Coady is founder and director of the Irish Film Festival . " alt= " ? ine Coady is founder and director of the Irish Film Festival . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;62106;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1153462 " /&gt; &lt;h3&gt;Vancouver Irish Film Festival&lt;/h3&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;When : &lt;/strong&gt; Nov. 29-Dec. 1 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Where : &lt;/strong&gt; Vancity Theatre &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **29;62136;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;virff.org&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; Back for its second year , the Vancouver Irish Film Festival ( VIRFF ) is returning with solid list of features , documentaries , and a new shorts program . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the Irish cinema culture is front and centre , the festival is also providing some non-film fun including traditional Irish music and dance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You don&amp;rsquo ; t have to be one of the 675,000 self-identifying Irish folk ( Stats Canada 2016 ) in the province to enjoy this festival . The films are first and foremost good . Many events sold out last year , so keep that in mind . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Postmedia News spoke to festival founder and director &amp;Aacute ; ine Coady about the growing event . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;62167;TOOLONG ... title= " ? ine Coady " alt= " ? ine Coady " data-portal-copyright= " Irish Film Festival " **28;62203;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1153459 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What is new in 2019 ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; This year we have introduced a shorts program featuring the best Irish shorts over the past decade . Irish short films have been incredibly successful on the world stage , having achieved two Academy Award wins and 10 Academy Award nominations over the last 15 years . &lt;em&gt; ( You can see the shorts on Nov. 30 at 1 p.m. at Vancity ) . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What was the impetus to start the festival ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I wanted to promote Irish film and Irish talent aboard and I saw an interest for a new film festival . There is such a wonderful Irish community in B.C. so I had brilliant support to launch it . We hope our schedule of programming will appeal not only to the growing Irish community in Vancouver but also to a broader audience . There are some amazing independent films in Ireland that , for a number of reasons , don&amp;rsquo ; t get seen in North America . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Storytelling and the Irish go hand in hand , so I imagine there was a lot of content to pick from . How did you decide on the lineup ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; The Irish have always had the gift of the gab and we love to tell a good story so it&amp;rsquo ; s little wonder that Irish talent have made some brilliant movies over the years . We started programming for this year&amp;rsquo ; s festival in July , choosing a mix of classic films and newer releases . As we are a new cultural festival , we could pick critically acclaimed films that had finished their distribution but may have not been seen in Canada . I was an independent filmmaker in Dublin before I moved here , so I know some crew who helped me with my indie programming . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How does the growing Irish population in B.C influence your programming choices ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; I wanted to appeal to all ages , so I feel the program at VIRFF 2019 has something for everyone : comedy , drama , fantasy , Irish Oscar shorts , documentaries , Irish-language cinema and family fare . I was delighted last year with the diverse audience of all generations , so we wanted to continue to grow on this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;62233;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;62269;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What film is you most excited to show to audiences ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Our opening gala film A Bump Along the Way is a new release to Irish cinemas . This female written , directed and produced film set in Derry is a charming feel-good film and I can&amp;rsquo ; t wait to see how audiences in Ireland react to it . Other program highlights include acclaimed documentary Gaza from Garry Keane and Andrew McConnell , and Ireland&amp;rsquo ; s official entry for best international film at the 2020 Academy Awards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What are some of the other , non-film programming you have planned for this year ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;A : &lt;/strong&gt; Festivalgoers can enjoy the &amp;ldquo ; craic , &amp;rdquo ; and watched live traditional Irish music and dancing performances . We have Irish food and drinks stalls , too , so everyone can enjoy an Irish cinema experience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All our volunteers ( including myself ) are Irish expats on a two-year working holiday visa , so we&amp;rsquo ; re working hard to bring Irish cinema to B.C. during our time in Canada . We don&amp;rsquo ; t have roots or family here but we wanted to leave an impact and establish a cultural film festival . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;62305;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " http : **34;62347;TOOLONG ... for something to do ? ArtsLocal NewsMoviesDisney+ premieres Noelle , its first holiday comedyhttp : **34;64166;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;64202;TOOLONG ... title= " Noelle ( Anna Kendrick ) and Snowcone. " alt= " Noelle ( Anna Kendrick ) and Snowcone. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;64238;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1133291 " /&gt; **45;64268;TOOLONG ... &lt;p&gt; **33;64315;TOOLONG : Nov. 12 , 2019 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;64350;TOOLONG : Disney+ &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tickets and info&lt;/strong&gt; : &lt;a href= " https : **28;64386;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **28;64416;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Noelle is the debut holiday comedy on the new Disney+ streaming platform . The feature-length film is a star-studded affair with a cast including Anna Kendrick , Bill Hader , Kingsley Ben-Adir , Billy Eichner , Julie Hagerty , Michael Gross , Ron Funches and film legend Shirley MacLaine . The film was shot in 2017 at Vancouver Film Studios and on a custom-designed North Pole village constructed in the Whistler area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The story of Noelle Kringle ( played by Anna Kendrick ) , the daughter of Kris &amp;ldquo ; Santa Claus&amp;rdquo ; Kringle , and her zany family is set in the weeks prior to Christmas . Things are all going according to plan , although complicated by changing family roles . Noelle&amp;rsquo ; s brother Nick ( Bill Hader ) is taking the reins of the flying sleigh on Dec. 24 for the first time . Caving under the pressure of all the responsibility , he vanishes , leaving his reluctant brother Gabe ( Billy Eichner ) with the job . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Noelle has to head to the Land of Nice , get Nick back in time and save Christmas . It means leaving the North Pole to play private detective in Phoenix . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Director Marc Lawrence ( Miss Congeniality , Two Weeks Notice ) wrote the story and took a lot of the inspiration for its look and feel from the thousands of holiday season images painted by American realist Thomas Kinkade . At a press visit to the North Pole town hall set in Burnaby , the wood and brass design both resembled a Kinkade cabin painting as well as hiding images of a certain mouse named Mickey here and there . The North Pole outside included an ice pond and numerous buildings that all had to be trucked to Whistler . Every one of the cast members assembled for the media day mentioned how incredibly cool the Maher Ahmad-designed set was . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anna Kendrick describes her character as a slightly spoiled , somewhat bored young lady who gets waited upon hand and foot by her 200-plus-year-old personal assistant Elf Polly ( Shirley MacLaine ) . Admitting that she is a fan of Christmas movies , Kendrick says the script passed her litmus test of &amp;ldquo ; was it a movie I would want to watch . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; My character flits around doing what she wants and , to be honest , she&amp;rsquo ; s pretty much loving it , &amp;rdquo ; said Kendrick. &amp;ldquo ; Her brother , Nick , isn&amp;rsquo ; t feeling the same way about things , but she figures she can bring him around in time . But then he vanishes , and she goes out to find him , and it turns out that Noelle finds her purpose along the way . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spoiler : It might have something to do with breaking the ice ceiling about which gender gets to go ho-ho-ho and deliver the goods . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During her adventures , many of the elements of the mythology around Santa Claus and Christmas are explained in amusing ways sure to bring a smile to children&amp;rsquo ; s faces . Kendrick was particularly pleased with the magic candy cane that makes chimneys get wide enough for Santa to fit down . She also loved the scenes shot skating on the ice pond . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Growing up in Maine , I had really white christmases , with a pond near my house that I could hobble over to and skate on , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; So I figured it would be like riding a bike to skate on the little pond on set , but it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t at all . Then I got the hang of it and I was really enjoying being able to skate over to the director to ask a question and skate back . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bill Hader was pleased to bring his brand of gawky comedy to the role of nervous Nick . He said his three kids were excited at the thought that he was going to Santa Claus&amp;rsquo ; house and , even after explaining that it was a set built to be the house , his daughter thought it was so cool that someone had gone to Santa&amp;rsquo ; s house to measure it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Nick is really stressed out about the whole Christmas thing , so he flees to Phoenix , Arizona , to become a yoga instructor , &amp;rdquo ; said Hader. &amp;ldquo ; Marc Lawrence wrote it that he does Christmas yoga , so it&amp;rsquo ; s things like the Christmas Tree pose , the reindeer pose and so on . Marc is a great writer with a very specific rhythm in his comedy and he has a very subtle way of working in the joke . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shirley MacLaine describes the script as &amp;ldquo ; adventurous and funny . &amp;rdquo ; Decked out in Elf clothes that she says aren&amp;rsquo ; t very far from what she might already wear , the Oscar-winning actor showed off her new Fluevog boots and kept everyone laughing . Other cast members were clearly somewhat in awe of her , picking her mind about everything from shooting Terms of Endearment to Golden Age greats such as Around the World in Eighty Days and Can-Can . As her interview exposed , MacLaine was clearly mining the cast for good material too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Renowned for being outspoken , she said that working with Marc Lawrence was rewarding , if &amp;ldquo ; very slow . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Marc&amp;rsquo ; s a real genius with a sophisticated , dry wit and we&amp;rsquo ; ll see if it works , &amp;rdquo ; said MacLaine. &amp;ldquo ; I love the idea of a female Santa Claus and working with Anna has been terrific , as I first knew there was something there when I saw her with George Clooney in Up in the Air . And Billy Hader is hilarious , I couldn&amp;rsquo ; t believe it when he said he used to be Arnold Schwarzenegger&amp;rsquo ; s personal assistant . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hader exposed the fact that he had been&amp;nbsp ; Schwarzenegger&amp;rsquo ; s PA when he was filming 2002s Collateral Damage in a 2014 interview with Andy Cohen on Watch What Happens Live . His impersonations of Arnold are captured on numerous video segments . Everyone starts somewhere . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Noelle arrives as Disney+ launches into the highly competitive world of film and TV streaming services . It&amp;rsquo ; s fair to say that a lot is riding on the feature to be a big hit and generate the kind of reviews and word of mouth that get customers clamouring for Disney+ in their own homes . MacLaine noted that it was such a profound difference to be involved in a film with this level of cast and budget that wasn&amp;rsquo ; t slated to go into theatres . She&amp;rsquo ; s as hooked into the digital domain as everyone else , admitting that her tablet is never far away . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The business today is all about these things in our hands , it has really shifted how we watch and what&amp;rsquo ; s getting made , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; The money is so much less dependent on getting that box office hit and that frees up the writing and the kind of stories being told . This kind of dry , fairytale comedy is brand new for me and it&amp;rsquo ; s interesting . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Disney+ launches on Nov. 12. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;64446;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;64482;TOOLONG Stuart DerdeynMon , 11 Nov 2019 17:18:06 **45;64518;TOOLONG ... ArtsMoviesNetflix aims for the best stories in global animationhttp : **31;65611;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;65644;TOOLONG ... title= " A still from the Netflix animated holiday feature Klaus . " alt= " A still from the Netflix animated holiday feature Klaus . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;65680;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1131836 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Melissa Cobb is vice-president for original animation at Netflix . Before taking on her present position in September 2017 , the industry veteran held senior management positions at the Walt Disney Company , Fox Feature Animation , and DreamWorks , where she was the producer of the Oscar-nominated Kung Fu Panda trilogy. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The UCLA School of Management MBA and Stanford BSc of California was in Vancouver recently to receive a Women in Animation Diversity Award , presented in partnership with the Spark Computer Graphics Society ( Spark CG ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;65710;TOOLONG ... title= " Melissa Cobb , Netflix VP original animation . " alt= " Melissa Cobb , Netflix VP original animation . " data-portal-copyright= " STEPHANIE GIRARD " **28;65746;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1131840 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before receiving that honour on the opening night of the SPARK Animation 2019 conference , Cobb took time to discuss the slate of animated features and series that are appearing on Netflix , and the changing face of how products are delivered to a global viewership. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While she was in Vancouver , Cobb visited Burnaby-based BRON Studios , where the Kris Pearn and Cory Evans-directed animated feature the Willoughbys is being produced . Hollywood North animation houses such as BRON Studios , Atomic Cartoons and others are actively involved in the creation of content for Netflix&amp;rsquo ; s massive schedule . As more OTT ( over-the-top ) streaming services enter the market , it&amp;rsquo ; s a sure bet that local animation houses will be cranking out new programming . Cobb said that receiving the diversity award reinforced the conscious effort that she and Netflix have made to promote women into all positions of an industry that has been accurately described as a &amp;ldquo ; pirate ship . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From females in top jobs to an incredible amount of mobility , the times are really changing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There has a been a big difference lately , with women running Paramount , DreamWorks , Warner Brothers and Nickelodeon , &amp;rdquo ; said Cobb. &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a really cool thing to see these people that I grew up with , even if we are in competition , being in these sorts of positions . The business has never been as fluid as it is now , with so much mobility for artists and others to be going to work on projects that they are excited about . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The idea being that , rather than sticking to the past practice of staying at the same studio for decades on end , creatives today are able to pursue their passion and get a paycheque . It translates into better quality work when the team is genuinely into the project . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a very international industry now , with new talent coming out of China , Ireland , Mexico , India and , of course , Canada , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; With the new models taking into account all the opportunities all over the world , the traditional route of coming through Hollywood to get a feature film made or be a serious creator is changing . There will be opportunities for new talent to emerge in regions and get supported in the country in which they live . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The decisions of what to program still roll out of Los Angeles , Calif. , but the amount of new , original content coming to the online broadcaster from other places is substantial . Talk to anyone at any major animation hub and they will tell you that the key to a really vital industry isn&amp;rsquo ; t in doing service work for others , but in creating your own intellectual property . Netflix has partnered with Hollywood North studios such as Atomic Cartoons to produce such popular original programs as Beat Bugs , The Last Kids , as well as service hits such as Hilda . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As well as North American products , Cobb has brought Mighty Little Bheem to Netflix . The first animated series from India follows the adventures of the baby version of the mythologically inspired character of Chhota Bheem , which has enjoyed multiple spinoff series . Nothing has been done to &amp;ldquo ; package&amp;rdquo ; the product for other markets either . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;65776;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;65812;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a non-verbal , preschool program that&amp;rsquo ; s entirely created in India that is now one of our biggest shows in that category globally , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; We don&amp;rsquo ; t judge what might work in a particular market , we make a piece of content , put it out everywhere , in every language and let the audience decide . We&amp;rsquo ; re finding that everybody gets it or doesn&amp;rsquo ; t , and what&amp;rsquo ; s the best is what rises to the top . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Every major service provider is pursuing new content too . Competition for original new programming is fierce and that&amp;rsquo ; s translating into good jobs , more variety in shows and &amp;mdash ; best of all &amp;mdash ; what Cobb describes as a &amp;ldquo ; smarter kid space . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is a lot of really smart writing and interesting work going on in kid&amp;rsquo ; s programs , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Once you&amp;rsquo ; ve opened up the doors to more creators around the globe and you are no longer looking at &amp;lsquo ; this needs to be for the 10 a.m. time slot&amp;rsquo ; anymore , things become a lot more exciting . You can take more risks , diversify more and bring in many more and varied voices . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is also the bottom line behind Netflix bringing in someone with Cobb&amp;rsquo ; s considerable chops to helm the kids-and-family fare . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Disney+ launches Nov. 12 , and the children&amp;rsquo ; s entertainment juggernaut pulled all of its movies from Netflix last year . Other content is expected to go away as different entertainment companies launch their own streaming services . The Magic Kingdom is beyond a formidable challenger to any and all firms that pursue a piece of the profitable younger-viewer segment . Cobb&amp;rsquo ; s division needs hits now , as Variety.com reports that 60 per cent of Netflix customers &amp;mdash ; or about 83 million households &amp;mdash ; regularly watch kids-and-family content . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;65848;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;65884;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; First up is director Sergio Pablos&amp;rsquo ; s holiday adventure comedy Klaus . The Spanish animated comedy is Netflix&amp;rsquo ; s first original animated feature and stars Jason Schwartzman , Rashida Jones , J.K. Simmons and Joan Cusack . It&amp;rsquo ; s a fictional origin story for the Santa Claus myth revolving around a friendship between a gung-ho postal worker who befriends a recluse who makes toys . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also on deck is the Willoughbys , an adaptation of a Lois Lowry novel about a family adventure directed by Pearn ( Cloudy With a Chance of Meatballs ) , which features a cast including Ricky Gervais and Martin Short . Jorge R. Guiti&amp;eacute ; rez ( Book of Life ) is producing a nine-episode limited series titled Maya and the Three based around Mesoamerican mythology , due to land in 2021 . Maya and the Three has a Canadian connection as work is being done on the show at Montreal&amp;rsquo ; s Tangent Animation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cobb says that animation is a major component of both family-and-adult , or prime time , Netflix content . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a huge shift in the industry , where we have our L.A. studios doing kids shows and the big features and the prime time division producing shows such as Big Mouth and other cool things , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Then we have our Tokyo division , which is producing 30-plus anime a year , which is a huge shift in that global anime market and very good for us globally . And then there is the stuff that falls outside of all that into special projects and so on . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With no commercial breaks , direct toy tie-ins and other typical marketing around Netflix kids-and-family programming , the streaming service is banking on a high volume of quality content to keep customers engaged . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **29;65920;TOOLONG " **36;65951;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **27;65989;TOOLONG " target= " blank " rel= " noopener " **29;66018;TOOLONG &l; ... &lt;/p&gt; Stuart DerdeynMon , 04 Nov 2019 19:00:17 **45;66049;TOOLONG ... Large posters of 10 major projects covered a wall near founder Richard Henriquez&amp;rsquo ; s office . Ever whimsical , he put the first tree atop a tower ( Eugenia Place , 1991 ) , and designed the ship-shaped 46-unit Dockside building beside Coal Harbour .
